# QHRRN-2 Design Ledger — Hypotheses vs. Proven Ground

**Purpose:** the single source of epistemic truth for this project. Every design element carries a status; statuses change only with dated, linked evidence. The discipline exists so that claims can never outrun measurement — every claim records which it is.

**Created:** 2026-07-19 · **Maintainers:** PI + Claude · **Companions:** `QHRRN2_Architecture.md` (the design the ledger governs); evidence for [R] entries is preserved in the local archive

---

## 0. Rules

**Status vocabulary:**

| Tag | Meaning | What it takes to claim it |
|---|---|---|
| **[P-C]** | Proven — constructive/mathematical | A proof or explicit construction in our setting (e.g., "identity is in the weight space", with the construction written down) |
| **[P-L]** | Proven — literature | A replicated, load-bearing published result we are *reusing, not extending* (cited) |
| **[P-M]** | Proven — measured by us | Our own probe/CI/experiment, with the artifact (script + date + number) linked |
| **[H-n]** | Hypothesis | An assumption we are betting on. **Must name its test** (CI gate or ablation row) and its falsification condition |
| **[R-n]** | Refuted | Contradicted by measurement or proof (archived preliminary study or later measurement) |
| **[S-n]** | Superseded | Dropped by design decision, not disproof — the reason recorded |

**Discipline (binding on all future work):**
1. No code implements an [H] without its named test existing in the same change.
2. Status changes are **append-only** in §5 with date + evidence link. Nobody edits history.
3. Every module header cites the ledger IDs it implements (e.g., `# Ledger: H3, P-C2`).
4. A failed [H] is a *reported negative result* in the paper, not a silent deletion.
5. Solve-rate claims live only in §3 (hypotheses) until eval day. Nothing in a README asserts performance.

---

## 1. Provenance — every load-bearing claim from the four source documents

### 1.1 *Renormalization of Thought* (Dec 8, 2025 — the theory notes)

| Claim (as stated) | Status now | Disposition in QHRRN-2 |
|---|---|---|
| Grid ≅ 2D lattice; pixels ≅ Potts fields ℤ_q | **[P-C]** trivially (a representation choice) | Kept: canvas + categorical fields |
| Rule ≅ Hamiltonian / transfer matrix | **[H-1]** (a modeling metaphor — useful, untestable as stated; operationalized via H-4/H-5) | Informs, doesn't constrain |
| Reasoning ≅ RG flow UV→IR to a stable fixed point; "applying the transformation again yields an invariant state" | **[H-2]**, sharpened: solved task = fixed point of the iterate map Y_{t+1}=F(Y_t). Test: convergence of recursion iterates on solved vs unsolved tasks (do solved tasks reach Y_{t+1}=Y_t?) | Kept as the recursion-with-deep-supervision design; fixed-point convergence becomes a *measured diagnostic* |
| Proposition 1: "All valid ARC objects are Area-Law states" (solid shapes carry information only on boundaries) | **[H-3]** — plausible for solid shapes; *false in general* (texture/noise-carrying tasks exist; identity requires full UV transmission — proven by an archived identity-transmission analysis + rank argument). Weakened form kept: *fine-scale locality is the right prior; information demand varies per task* | Replaced by the priced-stream mechanism: Area-Law is the **default the optimizer relaxes for a fee**, not an axiom |
| Disentangler Û acts **on the boundary between two blocks**, turning entanglement into local information | **[P-L]** as an architecture pattern (MERA staggering; Swin shifted windows) — note: same-block mixing provably degenerates to a tree ([R-2]) | Seam mixers, offset by (1,1) — the spec's own wiring, finally |
| Isometry Ŵ compresses blocks to conceptual tokens | **[P-L]** (coarse-graining works: all conv pyramids) — but *lossy-funnel sufficiency* is **[R-1]** | Kept as the *kept-channel*; complement goes to streams, not to /dev/null |
| Holographic loss L = −log P(Y\|X) + λ Σ_l \|γ_l\|·ln χ_l (RT: entropy = bonds cut × ln χ; G_N ∝ 1/χ) | Original form = **capacity** penalty on the architecture. **[S-1]**: superseded by the **usage** penalty (flux ledger I_s = measured nats crossing cut s, VIB bound) — the tighter MDL reading: capacity bounds usage, usage is what memorization actually spends. weight-spectrum entropy implements neither ([R-3]) | **[H-4]** (the central bet): pricing information flux across RG cuts improves few-shot generalization vs β=0. Tests: β-sweep, streams-off, flux-spectrum sanity (CI-5) |
| "Memorized solution = massive wormhole throat (high χ); reasoned = narrow throat" | Same as above — the qualitative prediction of **[H-4]**; directly measurable now: flux(identity) ≫ flux(constant-fill) is CI gate 5 | The tollgate design thesis |
| TTT = simulated annealing on free energy; P(z) ∝ e^{−βH_S(z)}; correct Langevin dz = −∇H dt + √(2T) dW | Annealing-as-frame: **[H-5]**. Note the doc's SGLD noise is the *correct* scale — mis-scaled implementations fail silently ([R-4]) | SGLD (correct scale) kept as an **ablation row only**; primary optimizer AdamW + LoO early stop |
| Melting → cooling → phase transition; "Translation Symmetry wins over Rotational Symmetry" (rule selection = SSB among candidate symmetries) | **[H-6]**: SSB is meaningful only with a discrete order parameter. Test: τ-annealed codebook; H[q] collapse correlates with correctness; ablation discrete-vs-continuous rule code | The rule codebook. continuous-weight variants are [R-5] |
| "Do not use PyTorch Linear; use a TN library" | **[S-2]**: superseded. The failure mode wasn't the library, it was unfalsified structure. We use JAX with *structural constraints + tests* instead of TN-library orthodoxy | — |
| Adaptive capacity: "if error is high, increase χ slightly" | **[H-7]** (untested to date): per-task capacity escalation (raise T, widen gates) when LoO fails. Test: escalation policy vs fixed, Phase 3 | Deferred to Phase 3; logged so it isn't lost |

Each maps to ledger IDs above.

Easy Sudoku = propagation side (cell-1: retention≈solve), hard Sudoku and ARC = search side (retention ≫ reachability). EqR's D/B recipe read mechanistically: depth = propagation, breadth = search.** — **QUANTITATIVE FORM (2026-08-23, wave 2; S5 strat-512 @t64, nested k-curve k=256): per-draw verified hit-rate decays geometrically with tdoku rating (.27 → .007 easiest→hardest octile) while vote@k climbs log-linearly in k on EVERY octile — vote@128 = 70.7 %, vote@256 = 80.7 % overall, hardest octile (rating ≥48, cold 0.5 %) 39 % @128 / 55 % @256, curve still rising (+10 pp per doubling); M1-on-breadth reached on the instrument set, full-test 20k confirmation = wave 3. The cold number and the breadth number DISSOCIATE across substrates: the T12 map (W2) is the best cold map (19.4 %) but a narrow funnel (.022/draw, vote@128 37.5 % on 20k); the T6 map (S5) is the wide funnel.** |

A5-class profile predictions use L1 ≤ .15.** | profile-distance + per-family retention on every new cell | a deviating cell shows no family-localized cost, or an on-profile cell shows one, at seeded n | continuous (each campaign) |

| **H-47** | Breadth–budget competition under the toll (registered 2026-08-27 from the Phase-B rung-1 data): at a fixed priced information budget, cold-optimality and draw-diversity COMPETE for the same nats — the fitting term spends the ~1k-nat allowance on cold precision and the multi-init endpoint diversity that breadth requires is priced out. The "competition for nats" mechanism is an interpretation-label (measured throat = training-objective flux, not an inference-time diversity ledger; a diversity-in-nats instrument is a noted future idea).** **PI CONJECTURE (2026-08-27, registered as predictions): toll effects correlate positively with scale — the precision/diversity competition eases as capacity grows; an optimal (β, d) point may exist. Adversarial note: the literal "more information through the toll at scale" contradicts Law 1 at fixed β (throat constant-to-declining); the survivable forms are (i) code-efficiency (same nats encode more structure at width) and (ii) knee-shift (optimal β falls with capacity). Registered as P-A (scale axis, fixed β): the d96 std-β priced arm's condensation ATTENUATES vs d64 — final screen-vb @256 > 27.0 with a non-flat k-curve; P-B (budget axis): the β/3 arm at d96 holds a wide funnel through late training. P-A alone → code-efficiency; P-B alone → budget-mediated competition (H-47 as registered); both → the conjecture's strong form and the (β,d) surface gets its first four corners; neither → priced breadth dead at these scales.** **ADJUDICATED (2026-08-29, rung 2): NEITHER FIRED → PRICED-BREADTH-DEAD at these scales; the PI conjecture's strong form does not hold. Sharpenings: (i) the d64 mid-training wide window did NOT replicate (C2@25k = 30.5 vs B3-d64@25k = 71.3) — 'width delays condensation' dies at n=2 (the d64 window was checkpoint luck or d64-specific); C2 is condensed-STATIONARY at all three d96 checkpoints. (ii) The registered kill ('a lower-β arm still condenses at matched width/steps') did NOT fire — C4 (β/3) GROWS monotonically and de-flattens (27.2→33.6→43.0; v256/v16 1.07→1.38) at near-β-inelastic throat (871→1319 train-nats, +51% for β÷3): the competition survives ONLY in its weak, slow, budget-mediated form, far below the P-B bar (42.97 vs 55). Consequence: priced funnels are not a ladder lane; the toll's measured roles = stability + compression + trainability ([[H-48]]); a β/6-class corner is a possible future (β,d)-surface point, not a rung-3 slot.** | rung 2: (i) β-ladder cell on the priced arm at width (β/3-class — does a larger budget let the funnel survive late training?); (ii) screens on ≥3 checkpoints per arm (the funnel-vs-training curve; does the priced funnel peak and fall while plain-FPA's grows?) | a lower-β priced arm still condenses (flat k-curve) at matched width/steps — the competition is not budget-mediated and the mechanism dies; or the priced funnel at standard β survives late training in a replication | Phase B rung 2 |

**Measurement law (2026-08-14, binding):** same-seed same-config replicates at d64 spread 10 retention counts and 3× on rg-radius (C-vs-C53 pair) — claim-bearing contrasts require n≥3 seeds or within-run pairing; single-run cross-run deltas under ~10 counts are non-evidence and are labeled so.

**Explicitly not hypotheses (settled):** that a low-rank linear funnel can't reason [R-1, P-M]; that weight-spectrum entropy is not holographic complexity [R-3, P-C]; that identity requires UV transmission [P-C, archived analysis]; that unmasked padded CE is gamed by background [P-M].

COLLAPSE CONTROL: the rule fired **COLLAPSED by its cold leg** (A7 11.18 vs W2 −8.2 pp) **but the mechanism is NOT H-45** — A7 retfm 1.00 and λ<1 at every monitor, val-best 14.87 @35k, and A7s1 = 20.18 ABOVE W2: at T12/50k the fixed point never degrades; what moves is cold reachability (→ H-46). FIX ARMS: A2 FIX-HOLDS (17.73, retfm 1.00 — settles contractive after a mid-training chaotic transient with λ spikes to 4.4); A3 FIX-PARTIAL (15.44; retfm 1.00 throughout; **tightest healthy seed pair 1.66 pp**; basins WIDENED: probe S(.4) = .96/.96 vs plain-T12 .62–.64 — the FPA mechanism works as designed); A4 FIX-FAILS at final (7.86; val-best 11.65 @10k) while its seed posts the record — eq_coupled is cold-capable, high-variance (13.3 pp pair spread), breadth-narrow; A9 (RI/NI+FPA) FIX-FAILS at final (2.79; val-best 15.46 @15k) — **the fixes do NOT compose**. PRICE CEILING: **PRICED-DEPTH-PAYS** (A5 13.50 vs W9 5.24, +8.3 pp); **PRICE-FLAT on budget at d32** (A6 11.09 vs W8 9.35, +1.7); all six priced arms stable (retfm 1.00) at I_total ≈ 1.0–1.6 k nats, A_total = 0 (≈250× compression); priced-d32 pair spread 0.68 pp. REGULARISER-OF-CHOICE = A2 (17.73 vs A5 13.50, margin 4.2 pp, labeled). **CONVENTIONAL-FAILS, and it is the wave's cleanest H-45 datum: A8 (plain T6 aug×500 + wd 1e-3 @50k) collapses on schedule — λ_joint crosses 1 at 10k, retfm <.9 at 15k (the registered precursor ORDERING confirmed), final retfm .27, cold 0.03 % — ×5 data and ×10 weight decay do not prevent the contractivity collapse; NI, price, FPA and training depth do.** SEED NOISE = 15.32 pp (A2 17.73 vs A2s1 2.40) — every registered cold contrast is WITHIN-SEED-NOISE as labeled; the pair spreads split by mechanism (FPA 1.7 / priced-d32 0.7 / RI+NI+FPA 2.7 vs RI+NI 15.3 / eq_coupled 13.3 / plain 9.0 / priced-T12 7.1). TRAJECTORY LAW: global Spearman(λ_joint, retfm) = −0.42 (n=160) — attenuated by restriction of range (8/16 arms never lose retfm; nothing to correlate); **within-arm, where an equilibrium exists and degrades, the law is strong: A8 −.79, A2 −.78, A4 −.84, A10 −.95**; the positive within-arm cases (A5 +.70, A7 +.68) are the pre-equilibrium formation phase (λ at a not-yet-fixed point is not a stability readout — priced arms pass through H-43's horizon phase at 5–15k then stabilize, the H-43→H-44 transition now visible INSIDE single trajectories); λ_joint on eq_coupled arms reads the expansive MASS mode (trained a1+a2 = 1.35/1.23; λ≈2 with retfm 1.00 — the 2026-08-14 gauge caveat realized as an instrument-contract limit). **RECIPE DECISION (mechanical, as registered): A2 = plain T12 + RI .5 + NI .01** (best val-selected cold among non-collapsed arms). **PHYSICS (full pass in the artifact):** (i) η endpoints stratify by mechanism — NI arms trained to η = 1.000/0.999/0.984 (EXACT replacement dynamics: the damping crutch removed, contraction forced into f itself; A2 settles self-contractive, A2s1 stays mildly expansive — the 15.3 pp seed spread is a PHASE-BOUNDARY phenomenon at d16, not jitter), FPA .71–.75, plain T12 .61–.76, priced .87–.93; (ii) NEW DIAGNOSTIC — the sign of (retfm − ret_sched) separates the pathologies: positive (A2 +.46, A9 +.90) = ramp-path turbulence with a contractive terminal map (benign, NI-class); negative (A8 −.19, A2s1 −.32) = true final-map degradation (the H-45 direction); (iii) funnel spectroscopy (per-draw hit-rate vs rating, log-slopes): S5 −0.058/rating with a fat tail (0.7 %/draw on the hardest octile — the funnel never closes) vs W2 −0.074, W3 −0.113 (huge easy-octile rates .55/draw, cliff beyond octile 3 — the registered informational prediction "W3 ≥ S5 at k=256" REFUTED: 30.3 vs 80.7; "W2 < S5" confirmed 44.7), priced ≤ −0.25 (no measurable tail at k=256) — **depth, RI/NI and price all concentrate the watershed onto easy instances; the wide funnel belongs to the shallow-horizon moderate-budget plain map**, and cold gains at T12 live almost entirely in the easiest octiles (A4s1 80.6 % on octile 1 vs S5 50.8; ~0 beyond octile 3); (iv) single-random-init vote@1 ≥ cold on all three PHASE4 arms (21.0/23.2/21.4 vs 17.7/21.2/20.2) — the H-37 deployment-distribution echo at 20k scale. **§3 UPDATES: H-45 scoped + mechanism-confirmed (annotation), H-44 annotated (priced-depth-pays, price-flat-at-d32, 250×-compression stability), NEW H-46 registered (cold-basin wander at fixed contractivity).** **CONSEQUENCES → PHASE B (the parity ladder, registered-unlaunched; launch registration with locked rules follows the build):** goal metric = 20k vote@128 @t64 (EqR like-for-like; bands B-M1 ≥.50 BANKED at d16 / B-M2 ≥.85 / B-M3 ≥.95) alongside cold M-bands; rungs d64 → d96 → d128 (--width-scale, all widths ∝ d, ≈5 M at d128 ≈ EqR's 5.03 M), T12, 50k, DP-8; rung-1 (d64) arms = A2-class (the registered recipe) + A3-class (FPA — the seed-stable alternate; one continues after rung 1 by val-selected cold + funnel width) + priced T12 (the stability/compression twin; PRICED-DEPTH-PAYS earns its slot); monitors every 2000 + banked ckpts every 5k + val-selected reporting (checkpoint variance is the dominant noise); **breadth scan per rung on BOTH the val-selected AND one mid-training banked ckpt (H-46's test: does the funnel narrow with training at scale?)**; Full suite green before the build; targeted suites green after. **PROTOCOL (binding, unchanged where not stated):** seeded 1k train × (1+100) group-aug, FULL 422,786-puzzle test EXACT at t∈{6,64} (cold, one deterministic prediction), strat-512 (`strat-seed 20260821`, the wave-1 set) @t∈{6,64,256} k=16, val@t64 (64 monitor rows) = M0-W2, retention@t8 (solution-init, strat-512), probe suite @t64 (origin layouts), breadth scan k=256 @t64 on S5/S4/S7 + S5 @t6 (strat-512), PHASE4 20k-subsample (`subsample-seed 20260822`) k=128 @t64 on each worker's best arm (95% CI ±0.7 pp at p=.5). **ARMS (d16 unless noted; plain = β 0/0; priced = knee 3e-5/1e-5; anchors p.3; B64; aug 100; seed 0):** W1 plain T6 @50k (budget; M0-W2 arm) · W13 plain T6 @100k (budget, 3rd point) · W2 plain T12 @30k +remat (training depth on the equilibrium base) · W3 plain T12 +RI .15 +NI .01 @30k +remat (the EqR recipe on the plain base) · W4 plain T6 d32 @20k +remat (width) · W8 PRICED T6 d32 @20k +remat (price × width) · W9 PRICED T6 d16 @50k (price × budget) · W6 β 3e-6/1e-6 T6 @20k (price DOSE = H-43's registered test) · W5 GEN: generator pretrain 20k (gen npz, aug 9) → STRICT finetune 20k via `--init-from` (plain T6; reported as its own labeled row) · W7 plain T6 @20k layout box4 (the registered box-aligned CONTROL) · W1s1, W4s1 seed-1 replicates (seed spread at both widths) · scans S5/S4/S7 @t64 k=256 + S5 @t6 k=256. Worker 0: W13 W2 W3 W1 W9 W5 W4 W8; worker 1: the 4 scans + W6 W7 W1s1 W4s1 (a v6e-8 runs both lists, 2 jobs/chip). The d32@50k corners (plain/priced) are DEFERRED to wave 3 contingent on W4/W8 (d32 arms are the wall-clock long pole at ~2–4× d16 for train AND eval); plain T24 likewise (0.6 it/s). **DECISION RULES (locked; analyzer header is the verbatim authority):** M0-W2: W1 val@t64 ≥ .20 → STRICT-TRAINABLE (STRICT restored as a co-primary labeled row), else GEN stays primary. HEADLINE = best cold full-test exact over all arms at t∈{6,64} → bands M1 ≥.50 / M2 ≥.85 / M3 ≥.95 (unchanged). BREADTH (labeled separately, never the headline): the best arm's 20k-subsample vote@128 (±CI; fallback S5's strat k-curve) → M1-ON-BREADTH iff ≥ .50. SEED NOISE = max(|W1−W1s1|, |W4−W4s1|, 1 pp); contrasts below 2×NOISE are labeled WITHIN-SEED-NOISE. P1 BUDGET: W1−S5 ≥ +5 pp → BUDGET-PAYS; ≤ +2 → BUDGET-NOT-BINDING; W13 ≥ W1 within noise → MONOTONE. P2 DEPTH: W2−S5 ≥ +3 AND retention(W2) ≥ .9 → DEPTH-PAYS; W3 vote16 − W2 vote16 ≥ +3 → RI+NI-RAISES-BREADTH. P3 WIDTH: W4−S5 ≥ +3 → WIDTH-PAYS. P4 PRICE×SCALE (H-44): each priced cell classed STABLE (ret ≥.9) / RECOVERED (stable AND cold ≥ 5 pp) / HORIZON (ret <.5 AND cold < 2 pp) / PARTIAL; H-44 SUPPORTED if W8 or W9 is STABLE-or-RECOVERED; FALSIFIED-AT-THESE-SCALES if both HORIZON; else INDETERMINATE; DOSE (H-43): W6 STABLE → "a 10× lower price keeps the equilibrium" (compression I_total S5/W6 reported), HORIZON → the dose kills too. P5 GEN: W5−S5 ≥ +3 → GEN-TRANSFERS; ≤ 0 → GEN-NO-TRANSFER. P6 BOX4: |W7−S5| ≤ 2 → GEOMETRY-NULL (predicted); ≥ +3 → GEOMETRY-MATTERS; ≤ −3 → BOX4-HURTS. P7 BREADTH SCAN: S5 vote@128 ≥ .50 → HOLDS; else SATURATED-BELOW-M1 if vote@256 − vote@128 < 2 pp; S7 hit-rate > S5 hit-rate → RI+NI-BREADTH (directional, H-37 under multi-init deployment). **REGISTERED PREDICTIONS (numeric):** W1 = S5 +5…+15 pp; W13 ≥ W1; W2 = S5 +3…+8 with ret ≥.9; W3 vote16 ≥ W2 vote16 +3; W4 = S5 +3…+8; W8 and W9 remain HORIZON maps at these scales (H-43) — the H-44 alternative is the PI's scale-dependence possibility, registered so that either outcome is a finding; W6 STABLE at 10–30× compression; W5 ≥ S5 +3; |W7−S5| ≤ 2; S5 vote@128 ≥ .50; best-arm 20k vote@128 ≥ .50 (M1-on-breadth); best cold ≈ 20–30 % (BELOW-M1 still expected on the cold number at this substrate — wave 2 targets M1 on breadth first, the climb continues with the winner). **[H-44] registered (§3 row):** the price dividend on CSP may EMERGE WITH SCALE — the knee-priced equilibrium loss (H-43, d16/20k) may attenuate or reverse as width/budget grow (the priced flux fraction becoming sufficient) — tested by W8/W9/W6 now and by d48/d64 priced cells later; falsified at these scales if both W8 and W9 stay horizon maps; supported if either holds an equilibrium. **COST/OPS:** 16 chip-jobs; v6e-16 ≈ $13.6/h spot, ETA ≈ 7–9 h absent churn (long poles W13 @100k and the d32 arms' evals) ≈ $110–130; v6e-8 fallback ≈ 2× wall at ≈ $6.8/h (same dollars); WALL 8.5 h per launch (relaunch resumes); tag `sport2w2`, GCS `sport2w2`; `tools/campaign.env` ← `campaign_sport2w2.env`; supervisor + heartbeat + three monitors; HANDOFF current-campaign block = wave 2; analysis = `analyze_sport2w2.py` untouched, reserved for the analysis pass. Wave-3 follow-ons (registered-unlaunched, competing under guardrail 1): d32@50k plain/priced if W4/W8 warrant; plain T24; seeds ×2 on the winning config; FPOpt γ on the best arm; d48/d64 priced cells for H-44 if W8/W9 read STABLE. Goal metric named (guardrail 1): Sudoku-Extreme full-test exact (cold) + the labeled breadth number.**

- **2026-08-22 (SPRINT S2 VERDICT — Sudoku-Extreme wave 1, adjudicated by `tools/analyze_sport2.py` run untouched against the 2026-08-21 registration (artifact `runs/analysis/sport2_verdict.txt`); report `Documentation/Report_2026-08-22_SprintS2_Verdict.md` (committed 723b4e2; this entry completes it). Post-results critique clean: n=422,786 on every full-test eval, verified≡true multi-init hits everywhere, valid-but-wrong ≈0, all 8 ckpts at registered d/T/β/NI (RI/digit-aug flags unverifiable at artifact level — config.json not in the tarball; both null-result arms), the uniform ~2.2% at t=6 = a shared rating-0 easy tail (7,955 puzzles, all rating 0), training curves pulled from GCS.)** — **HEADLINE: BELOW-M1. Best arm S5 (plain β=0, T6) at inference depth t=64: 12.02% exact on the full test (strat-512 14.1%; t=256 14.3%); verify-and-vote k=16: 36.9% (t=64) / 37.7% (t=256) on the 512 set, labeled separately. S4 (T24 priced) 10.86%, S7 (RI+NI+T12) 6.19%, S3 1.11%, S2 1.64%, S0/S1/S6 ≤0.17%. M0 FAIL (val monitor 0/64 on every arm but S4 4/64; train loss .50–.62 at 20k — the 1k regime is UNDER-TRAINED at 20k steps, ~1/50 of TRM's budget) → registered consequence: the GEN row becomes the primary labeled row in wave 2, STRICT still reported. P1 FLAT (RI alone at priced T6 .009→.013); P2 HALF-FAILED informatively (inference depth monotone on 3/8 arms — S4/S5/S7 rise ×5, S0/S1/S3/S6 collapse, S2 explodes; training depth S4 +10.8 pp); P3 HOLDS (S6−S0 −0.08 pp: exact S9 covers digit aug); P4 REVERSED — PLAIN ≫ PRICED by 11.94 pp (→ H-43); P5 HOLDS (S7 6.19 vs 1.64). BRANCH RULE fired by its letter (below M1 → box-aligned first) but its PREMISE is not met (breadth tried only at k=16, depth never at β=0, training 1/50 of TRM) and its DIAGNOSIS is refuted by the instruments (failures are near-misses at every rating: median 3 violations, givens kept 99.5% — a SEARCH failure, not box geometry) → box-aligned runs as one control arm in wave 2, not as the lever.** **THE FINDING (paper 1's spine, sharpest form): the retention/reachability dissociation RESOLVED BY DIFFICULTY. S5 by tdoku rating on the 512 set: retention .95/.98/.99/.97/1.00 at ratings 0 / 1–9 / 10–29 / 30–59 / 60+ (basins exist at EVERY difficulty; S(.2)≈1.0), cold solve 64 / 17 / 1 / 0 / 3%, verify-and-vote k=16 91 / 56 / 20 / 16 / 9% with ~1 hit/16 on the hard bins (breadth has large headroom; EqR's B=128). Basin existence is difficulty-independent; reachability ∝ 1/required-search; breadth with free verification recovers it. Easy Sudoku (cell-1) = propagation side, hard Sudoku + ARC = search side; the benchmark's own difficulty metric is a proxy for our reachability gap; EqR's D/B = propagation/search. 81% of S5's solves need >6 steps (cell-1's depth-limitation reproduced on the benchmark).** **PHYSICS: (1) [H-43 registered] the price KILLS equilibria on CSP — priced T6 maps are HORIZON maps (≈2.2% at t=6 like every arm, then degrade: retention ≈0, givens kept 100→86→63% across t 6/64/256); the plain T6 map is an EQUILIBRIUM map (retention .98, improves 2.3→12.0% with depth); depth T24 or RI+NI at T12 restore equilibrium under price (S4 ret 1.00, S7 1.00; S3 T12-alone .01); NI alone at T6 is explosive (flux 1e10). The pricing dividend is DOMAIN-DEPENDENT (H-4 scoped; Law 4 ARC-only): rule+transport tasks gain, working-state tasks pay. (2) η separates horizon (→1) from equilibrium (~.55–.65) maps (S5 .555, S4 .650 vs S0 .904, S1 .980; S7 .944 the exception). (3) breadth pays through start-state trajectory diversity (evaluator's full-schedule multi-init 37%) not final-map basin capture (probe mi ≈1% on every arm) — H-37's mechanism, again. (4) throats: priced 1.7–2.0k nats UV .93–.97 (a third fixed profile, the most UV-concentrated measured), plain 302k UV .86. ADVERSARIAL: n=1 seed/arm (only the enormous effects carry claims: plain-vs-priced 12.0 vs 0.08, retention .98 vs .01, breadth ×3 at n=512); the substrate is weak (78k params, 20k steps, M0 FAIL) — wave 1 was a mechanism ladder, not a climb; verify-and-vote is a separate protocol from the series' single-prediction number (always labeled); M0-at-t=T is an insensitive gate (wave 2 reads val at t=64); full t=256 not run (strat: +0.2 pp over t=64). **WAVE 2 DESIGN (goal = the bands; every arm names its expected effect): (0) breadth scan k∈{16,64,128,256} on banked S5/S4 at t=64, eval-only first hour — prediction: S5 vote ≥50% at k=128 (M1 on the breadth number); then the full-test vote at the best affordable k (or ≥20k-puzzle subsample w/ CI); (1) plain T6 50k-step full schedule (budget; +5–15 pp predicted); (2) plain T12 (+remat) and plain T12+RI+NI (≥S5, +3–8; RI+NI raises breadth hits); (3) plain T6 d32 (capacity); (4) GEN: generator pretrain → 1k finetune via --init-from (M0 consequence; ≥STRICT, own labeled row); (5) β=3e-6 plain-T6 (H-43's test); (6) box-aligned representation (registered control; ≈ plain T6 ±2). Wave-2 M0 = val at t=64. ≈8 arms, one pod-day ≈ $60–80 (T6 50k ≈3 h wall, T12 30k ≈4.5 h; T24 deferred — 0.6 it/s wall).**

Deadlines VERIFIED today against the CFPs: ICLR 2027 abstract Sep 18 / full Sep 25 AOE; AAMAS 2027 abstract Oct 2 / full Oct 9 — paper 1 → ICLR (M2 by ~Sep 15) else AAMAS; paper 2 → AAMAS (the original Sep-28 freeze becomes paper 2's) else ICML (late Jan, to verify). Venue = outcome (one-plan amendment stands). **THE SERIES AS THE SPINE (PI directive): HRM (27M, 1k-example regime, assumed equilibria + 1-step IFT gradient, deep supervision/ACT; Sudoku-Extreme ~55% per TRM's table) → TRM (7M, drops hierarchy + the equilibrium assumption, full BPTT through recursion; Sudoku-Extreme ~87% MLP-T / ARC-1 ~45%) → EqR (5.03M, makes equilibria REAL: stabilized attractors, RI/NI training, depth D + breadth B test-time scaling; Sudoku-Extreme 99.8% @ D=64 B=128) → FPRM (7M, stabilizer theory: pre-norm, coupled residual scaling, damped iteration, residual halt, FPOpt, Neumann-K; ARC-1 47.5% no-TTT).   - *Same-day addendum (PI clarification, paper-1 IDENTITY — binding on the contribution statement and on the sprint design):* paper 1 is NOT a measurement-tools paper. Its object is the **physics-informed architecture itself** — priced information flow through RG cuts (the flux ledger / VIB streams, knee pricing, free-bits), the equilibrium core with basin objectives, exact S9 symmetry, and NI/RI/anchors as *measured-in* mechanisms — plus the instruments that close the loop **measure → understand → improve**, which the ledger documents as the program's actual design method (E3b truth-erasure → E10 equilibrium core, retention 6%→29%; C.2 erosion → basin-preserving TTT; rung-0 transfer variance → NI in; H-39 → floors out; H-40/41 → budgets held; H-37 → multi-init deployment; anti-Arrhenius → cold candidates; near-miss coverage → keyhole+snap). And the same instruments distinguish ARC from Sudoku, which no one in the series does. CONSEQUENCE FOR SPRINT S2: the loop is DEMONSTRATED, not asserted — S0's instrument readouts (violations-at-depth, multi-init hit rate, retention, condensation, spectra) are read FIRST and used to pre-register which mechanism arms should pay and by how much, before those arms run (the H-37 pattern, made the sprint's method); priced-vs-plain (S5) is a central arm, not an ablation afterthought.

H-34 TWO-PROFILE STRUCTURE HOLDS (all priced d64 on knee ≤.061, plain on free) — third seeded test; the knee profile is a width-invariant fixed point, but NOT a sufficient health indicator (on-profile cells lost N diffusely: CompleteShape −2.3, HorizontalVertical −2.0, Order −2.0, TopBottom2D −1.3, InsideOutside −1.0 at 9-pair resolution). **OTHER STANDING CLAIMS:** S1 EXTENDS (throat + IR conservation); S2 HOLDS (plain inflates 1.50× with width, 209k→313k nats, and holds fewer basins; priced vs plain 600× flux / 1.8× codewords / 2.5× transfer); Law 2 HOLDS (N moved, r flat); Law 3 RESTATED (code distance saturates by d48 at this price/depth — with steps ∝ d, r̄ flat and N falls; 'width keeps paying' does NOT hold at d64); H-31 localization 2nd sighting (NI tax at d64 +18 nats UV-concentrated [+9.6,+8.5,+1.3,+3.5,−4.5], A5−C53 n=1; d48 clean +12); H-32 KILL STANDS and the sign may reverse (sub-floor A5 = best transfer); H-35 FALSIFIED as a width effect (Center ~1–2/9 at both widths seeded; the '5/9 at d48' was the single p1248c40k cell); VACANCY FLOOR holds at seeded d64 — Copy/ExtractObjects 0/9 on all six arms (0 on 37 substrates), Count 1/9 once; REACHABILITY WALL holds and worsens slightly (e3 wrong-stable .91–.94 of converged vs .88–.90; exact/N .20–.31 vs .28–.34; nd=1 endpoint condensation 33–44 vs 49–52 — width adds distinct, mostly wrong, endpoints; exact-when-reached is a fixed point: exact&not-retained 0/144 in every d64 cell). **ADVERSARIAL (full list in the report §4):** the ceiling is protocol-bound (fixed β=3e-5 calibrated at d16, fixed floor vector, T6, linear steps) — the β dial carries a mechanism (rate–distortion at fixed β: more compressible codes at width/budget migrate to lower rate and pay in unseen-family basins; C80 n=1 shows it within an arm) and a cheap discriminating cell; A5 n=1 (all R1-4 statements directional, 9.6-count margin at the law's threshold); per-family numbers are 9-pair counts (only aggregates are claim-bearing); plain d64 n=2 vs d48 spread 12 (in-distribution plain deficit not established); two I_med conventions exist (this entry: median of totals; the 08-14 physics pass C53 443 = sum of medians); wave-2 comparators lack rb-48 (compare rg-48 only). **CURIOSITY (report §5):** (1) the width deficit grows with distance from the training distribution — rt-48 flat (27 vs 26, n=1), vh −14% (n=3), rg-96 −28% (n=3): capacity at fixed β specializes the code to trained family structure; (2) floors became BINDING at d64 (A4 pinned at the floor vector; A5 below on 4/5 cuts and best) — the floor vector is a d48/wave-2 constant (H-39); (3) over-compression at long budget C53→C80 (n=1): −20 nats, N −4, rg-48 −6, exact +3 (H-38); (4) IR conservation: s3+s4 ≈ 45–50 nats at both widths — a candidate direct measurement of S1's floor; (5) spurious-attractor proliferation with capacity (e3); (6) pricing buys existence not reachability at every width (absolute exact ≈ equal priced vs plain; priced holds 1.7–1.8× more codewords); (7) η sign reversal plain≥priced at d64. **REGISTERED CONSEQUENCES (PI decision; nothing launched): H-38 β-rescale cell at d64 (A5-class, β 1e-5/3e-6 + 3e-5, seeds ×2, ~1.5 pod-h) — decides pricing-artifact vs geometric BEFORE the ladder commits d96+ resources; H-39 A5 seeds ×2 (~1.3 pod-h); depth-lean rung 2 at d48 (T8/T12, steps ∝ T·d, A5-class ×3) if H-38 reads geometric; rt on every seed next cell; d48@53k one-seed η piggyback. Rung-2 registration RE-OPENED (the 08-14 ladder's d72/d96 branch is not taken). Basin Measurement Note: L1/L2/L4 extend-or-hold, L3 restated, η dictionary entry (0.058) to be updated — full claim table in the report §6. Rung-1 cost $65.74; program ≈ $459.**

NI's per-step noise appears to REGULARIZE transfer geometry — averaging over the idiosyncratic basin placements that make single seeds scatter. That is a stabilizer, measured at n=3, and it is why A4 is the operating substrate. All 12 arms trained to eta .16-.19 — priced AND plain, against predicted bands of .22-.35 (priced) and <.12 (plain). Both bands missed in the same direction because H-30 conflated variables the seeded grid now separates: (i) DEPTH SETS eta, not price — at matched (d, budget, priced) higher T gives LOWER eta every time (d32/20k: T4 .167 vs T6 .136; d48/40k: T4 .244 vs T6 .181), each step doing less as refinement spreads over more iterations; (ii) THE PRICING EFFECT ON eta DECAYS WITH WIDTH — plain vs priced: d16 .047/.148 (3.2x), d24 .072/.144 (2.0x), d32 .102/.167 (1.6x), d48/T6 .168/.181 (1.08x). Pricing raised eta where the substrate was narrow; at width the free map flows fast on its own. RESTATED H-30 (registered now, testable at rung 1): eta = f(width, depth) with the price effect vanishing as width grows; predicts d64/T6 priced ~ .24 (matches C53/Dfloor .24!) and plain d64/T6 within ~10% of it. The 'compressed landscapes are coarser and flow faster' physics reading survives only for narrow substrates.** **(R6, OPERATING SUBSTRATE for rung 1 = A4 floors+NI): rg96(ret+S2) 44.3 vs A1 31.7 / A2 30.3 / A3 21.0; vh S(.4) 21.3 (best); vh count 47.3 (best); exact 14.3 (best). A4 wins every ranked metric and its transfer is seed-invariant. Since R1 showed floors are inert, the working hypothesis is that NI alone would reproduce A4 — a global+NI arm is the one-flag decomposition owed at rung 1 (registered).** **ADVERSARIAL PASS — what these numbers do NOT show: (1) my own over-narration caught: I initially read the plain arm as 'decoding truth it cannot hold' — the data refute it (plain exact&retained 26 vs exact&not-retained 1; priced 34/3 and 41/2) — exact-when-reached is a genuine fixed point in ALL arms; the S-port's decode-vs-hold dissociation does NOT appear on ARC at this scale; struck. (2) Two analyzer branches were mis-specified (R1 harmful-without-significance; R2 ws-drop at count resolution) — corrected above; the analyzer is a tool, the ledger is the verdict. (3) n=3 seeds is the measurement law's minimum; the seed spread on S(.4) (~2.5) and vh retention (~2-4) bounds every within-priced contrast — the priced-vs-plain contrasts (40% retention, 400x flux, 6/6 transfer) are far above it; the floors-vs-global and NI-retention contrasts are AT it. (4) Wrong-stable is ~124/144 in every arm — convergence is near-universal (no-converge 36->7 since pretrain8) but limits are still ~86% wrong: the reachability wall is UNCHANGED by anything in rung 0. (5) rg-96 doubles resolution over rg-48 but per-seed counts 15-35/288 are still low; the A4 seed-invariance (spread 1) is the single most surprising number here and deserves a 4th seed before it carries weight in the paper. (6) VACANCY FLOOR at n=3 x 4 arms: Copy and ExtractObjects 0/27 in EVERY arm — now zero on 31 substrates total; Count 1-3/27. Nothing in rung 0 touched it; it remains the program's hardest invariant and its clearest architectural limit (content-verbatim families get no basins). (7) The T4-vs-T6 comparison at d48/40k rests on n=1 for T4 (p1248c40k) — the depth axis needs a seeded T4 arm before 'depth does not change the throat' is more than directional.** **REGISTERED CONSEQUENCES FOR RUNG 1 (d64 x 3 seeds, the H-36 width-ceiling test): operating arm = A4 (floors+NI); ADD one global+NI seed (floors decomposition); T4 seed for the depth axis; rg-96 stays; H-30 restated prediction (priced d64/T6 ~ .24, plain within 10%) is a live falsifiable test at rung 1. The hunter's network-vs-preemption bug (stray pod, 08-15) must be fixed before hunters run again: an empty/errored describe is 'unknown, retry', never 'preempted, tear down + re-create'.**

- **2026-08-15 (S-PORT CELL-1 COMPLETE — both arms adjudicated + full construction/run/results audit (independent second-pass review of the overnight build) + the REGISTERED DEPTH-SWEEP FOLLOW-UP EXECUTED AND CONFIRMED. Every number below independently RE-DERIVED from raw rows by a second script (scratchpad sport_rederive.py) — the analyzer's figures reproduce exactly; two small corrections to the sudA entry are recorded at the end. H-33 RESOLVES AS A SPLIT: its landscape-class claim is CONFIRMED for basin EXISTENCE, its central RI mechanism is FALSIFIED AS REGISTERED and replaced by a sharper, doubly-supported law — RI pays exactly on ITS OWN INIT DISTRIBUTION, in both domains.)** — **AUDIT SUMMARY (construction + runs, verified at code and checkpoint level): treatment REAL — build_y0_rows is corpus-agnostic (pretrain.py:276), so anchors (p=.3, eps=.15) trained in BOTH arms and RI (.15) in sudB on the Sudoku path; configs from ckpts: both d16/T6/K64/knee-beta/seed-0/20k, differing ONLY in ri_p; eval genuinely held out — 0/120 eval puzzles AND 0/120 eval solutions appear among the 8,000 training pairs (measured, not assumed); pairing exact — identical task ids, n_givens, and multi-init draws across arms (McNemar valid); n_givens hits target exactly at every level (no punch floor-overshoot); retention rows binary in sudA, sudB shows 2 RECAPTURE rows at 30 givens (off-solution at step 1, back by 2 — the less-damped map's transient; see eta below).** **(S-R2, THE RI VERDICT — the registered kill FIRES on the registered metric: cold-start solve is NULL at every level (50: 39-vs-39; 40: 36-vs-34, p=.625; 30: 4-vs-6, p=.625; pooled 79-vs-79 of 120, p=1.0; val curves end identical 47/64) — so on a single-attractor landscape RI still buys NO deployed accuracy, and H-33's central mechanism (landscape class determines RI's dividend) is DEAD AS REGISTERED. BUT the paired multi-init data refute the lazy redundancy reading (the 'anchors already do this' reading floated during the overnight run — never ledgered, now measured wrong): RI STRONGLY reshapes convergence from RANDOM states, with a stark difficulty crossover on identical paired draws — per-puzzle hits/16 at 40 givens: sudB median 11 vs sudA 3 (B>A on 38/40 puzzles, sign p≈1e-10); at 30: 3.8x rate (.034 vs .009); at 50 REVERSED: A 8 vs B 4 (A>B 35/36, p≈1e-9). THE REPLACEMENT LAW (cross-domain, both results now consistent): RI training buys robustness ON ITS TRAINING INIT DISTRIBUTION — it converts random-init convergence, not cold-start accuracy; ARC's Dri (endpoint condensation without coverage) and Sudoku's sudB (mi-rate doubling without solve change) are the same phenomenon. THE RECONCILIATION WITH EqR: their INFERENCE protocol initializes from random states (multi-init breadth is their deployment), ours initializes from VOID — RI 'pays' exactly when deployment draws from RI's training distribution. Registered as [H-37] in §3 with its zero-compute supporting data and a named live test (verify-and-vote multi-init inference at 40 givens: predicts sudB ~2.3x sudA per draw; Sudoku solutions are checkable, so verification is free). The 50-given reversal is REPORTED UNEXPLAINED (strongly paired, opposite-signed; candidate probes named: per-draw endpoint distances, 2-cycle detection at steps 7-vs-8).** **(DEPTH SWEEP — the sudA-entry follow-up, run as registered on the 30-given stratum, one 64-step trace per puzzle, exact@t read per step: 3-4/40 @ t=6 -> 17/40 @ t=16 -> 19/40 @ t=32 -> 20/40 @ t=64; median violations 7 -> 1; ZERO over-iteration losses. DEPTH-LIMITATION CONFIRMED: half the hard-level failures were pure step starvation. THE CROSS-DOMAIN DISSOCIATION IS NOW MEASURED WITH THE SAME INSTRUMENT: ARC horizon probe (2026-08-09) t=96 = +0 exact (landscape-limited — basins absent); Sudoku t=64 = +16 exact (depth-limited — basins present, propagation unfinished). This is the program's cleanest two-sided result: the extended-iteration instrument SEPARATES failure classes across domains, and EqR/FPRM's enormous unrolls are mechanistically justified for constraint-propagation tasks and mechanistically USELESS for inventory-limited ARC — our instruments explain both fields' architecture choices. The residual 50% at 30 givens saturates (32->64 adds +1) at NEAR-SOLUTIONS (median 1 violation from valid): on a single-solution domain these are SPURIOUS ATTRACTORS — which makes NI's fewer-spurious-attractors claim finally testable in its home regime (candidate S-port cell: +NI arm, readout = stuck-at-viol>=1 rate at depth; registered-unbuilt).** **(NEW SUBSTRATE PHYSICS from re-derivation, absent from the sudA entry: (1) THE THROAT IS INSTANCE-CONDITIONAL — median I_total per level: 801 (50g) -> 987 (40g) -> 1528 (30g) nats on the SAME substrate and map: harder instances ROUTE MORE INFORMATION, so on Sudoku the flux ledger reads out required computation per instance, unlike ARC where I_med behaved as a task-set constant; the sudA entry's 'throat 987' silently pooled difficulty levels and should be read per-level. UV share stays .87-.89 with mild IR-fattening at 30g (scale-1 share 7.6% -> 10.1%) — more global coordination on harder instances. (2) eta: sudA .956, sudB .989 — RI pushes the flow constant TOWARD REPLACEMENT dynamics, the third domain-independent sighting (ARC Dri .241->.323); near-eta=1 also explains sudB's recapture transients (2-step settling). (3) The corruption ladder is FLAT at 40/40 through eps=.2 at ALL levels incl. 30g where cold-start solve is 10% — basins are wide and present everywhere; the watershed failure is strictly between VOID-start and 20%-corrupted-solution: C.2's retention-vs-reachability dissociation reproduced under a controlled difficulty dial.)** **CORRECTIONS to the 2026-08-14 sudA entry (append-only discipline; the entry stands, these amend it): cells_med at 30 givens is 73 (the 72 was the failed-rows-only median, mislabeled); 'throat 987 nats' is the pooled-difficulty median — per-level values above are the meaningful figures. INSTRUMENT CAVEAT (named): probe (trace_flux) and depth-sweep (P.trace) disagree on exactly 1/120 rows at t=6 (g30_0016, a boundary case) — the two eq-trace paths are NOT bit-identical; named test owed (trace-equivalence gate) before any claim rides a single marginal instance; no present verdict moves (3-vs-4 of 40). PILOT SCOPE (binding on every claim above): 77k params vs EqR/FPRM's ~5M, generator puzzles at a difficulty dial (NOT Sudoku-Extreme; no comparison to their 99.8% permitted), n=1 seed per arm, k=16 multi-init, eval n=40/level; per-draw mi rates are within-puzzle correlated (the paired per-puzzle sign tests above are the honest statistics). The cell cost ~$2.63 total and each conclusion names its follow-up. OPS one-liner: overnight = 6 preemptions all auto-recovered (hunter+durability stack), S-port chain self-completed and self-tore-down; two tooling bugs found+fixed in review (watcher exit-on-absence; a /tmp-cwd relative gsutil pull that silently landed sudB's ckpt outside the repo — the analyzer's silent-skip of missing eta inputs is itself now a noted anti-pattern).**

- **2026-08-14 (S-PORT CELL-1 — sudA VERDICT — INTERIM, SINGLE-ARM; SUPERSEDED BY THE 2026-08-15 COMPLETE VERDICT ABOVE. Read the 08-15 entry as authoritative: it re-derives every figure, adds sudB and the executed depth sweep, records two corrections to this entry (cells_med at 30 givens = 73; 'throat 987' was a pooled-level median), and REPLACES this entry's over-read of the multi-init result ('path-independence without RI') with the paired sudA-vs-sudB data. Kept verbatim below under append-only discipline.) (original title: arm 1 of 2; sudB/RI still training, S-R2 pending): H-33 SPLITS BY DIFFICULTY, and the split is a BETTER result than the hypothesis — the retention/solve dissociation COLLAPSES where the required computation fits the substrate's depth and RETURNS where it does not, on the SAME domain, substrate and instruments. Three registered cross-domain riders all read OUT-OF-BAND, which is itself the finding: two of our "laws" are ARC-landscape-specific, not architectural)** — Data: runs/sudprobe_sudA (120 rows = 3 paired difficulty levels x 40 puzzles, held out at a different eval seed), substrate runs/sud_sudA (d16/T6, 20k steps, knee-priced, anchors .3, 8000 generated puzzles @ 30-50 givens, ONE task row, NO TTT). Analyzer tools/analyze_sport.py (written pre-data, 7/7 synthetic rule checks). **THE TABLE (givens / solve / retention / viol_med / cells_med / givens-kept / multi-init hit-rate): 50 -> 97.5% / 100% / 0 / 81 / 100% / .494 | 40 -> 90% / 100% / 0 / 81 / 100% / .256 | 30 -> 10% / 100% / 7 / 72 / 100% / .009.** **(S-R1) CONFIRMED at 50 (ratio 1.03) and 40 (1.11) givens; the KILL branch fires at 30 (10.0). READ CAREFULLY, this is not a contradiction: RETENTION IS 100% AT EVERY DIFFICULTY — the map holds a handed solution perfectly whether the puzzle had 50 clues or 30, because the rule is universal and the basin therefore always EXISTS. What tracks difficulty is REACHABILITY from a cold start (97.5 -> 90 -> 10%). So the dissociation decomposes into TWO independent factors that ARC alone could never separate: (i) BASIN EXISTENCE — a landscape-class property (ARC 29%, Sudoku 100%); (ii) BASIN REACHABILITY GIVEN EXISTENCE — a COMPUTATIONAL-DEPTH property (ARC 5.6/29.2 = 19%; Sudoku 97.5% at 50 clues, 10% at 30 clues at the SAME T=6). H-33's landscape-class claim is CONFIRMED for factor (i) and REFINED for factor (ii): the ARC retention-without-solve gap is now a candidate instance of depth-limitation rather than a landscape fact, and that is a testable reinterpretation of the program's oldest wall (registered follow-up: inference-depth sweep t_total in {6,16,32,64} on the 30-given stratum — if solve rises with steps, depth-limitation is confirmed and EqR/FPRM's deep unrolls are the named fix).** **(S-R3) DEGENERATE-BY-SUCCESS and reported as such: every failed puzzle at every level is a with-basin puzzle (retention 100%), so the without-basin stratum is EMPTY and the ARC 31x enrichment ratio has nothing to condition on. The power guard fired correctly (no ratio claimed). In spirit this is H-33-iii's strongest possible form — basin existence stops being the bottleneck entirely — but the registered ratio test is undefined here and no number is claimed.** **(S-R4, the cross-domain riders — both OUT OF BAND, both informative): (a) H-30 FLOW-VELOCITY: trained eta = 0.956 vs the ARC priced-20k band .14-.19. Sudoku's optimizer chose near-total replacement per step (y <- y + .96(p-y)), ARC's chose slow quasi-static flow. The band is ARC-SPECIFIC; the law's honest form is that eta tracks the LANDSCAPE's required refinement style, not price/budget alone — H-30 as written is strained by its first out-of-domain test and must be restated (a priced-40k+ ARC cell can still be predicted; a cross-domain cell cannot). (b) H-34 SPECTRAL PROFILE: Sudoku's normalized profile is [.894/.077/.024/.004/.0004] — d(knee) .407, d(free) .269, i.e. NEITHER ARC fixed point. It is far MORE UV-concentrated than even the unpriced ARC profile: 89% of the information sits at the finest scale, which is exactly right for a purely LOCAL constraint problem with no coarse structure to carry. THE TWO-FIXED-POINT PICTURE IS THEREFORE TASK-DISTRIBUTION-DEPENDENT, not universal — a THIRD profile exists, and the profile is a readout of the task's information geometry rather than of the architecture. Throat 987 nats (vs ARC d16's 785).** **FREE COROLLARIES: (1) givens-preservation is 100% at every level — the dynamics never overwrite their own boundary condition, so failures are pure completion failures, not evidence-destruction. (2) The domain-native graded metric earns its place immediately: failures at 30 givens sit at 72/81 cells and 7 violations — NEAR-MISSES, not collapses, which is precisely the signature of a propagation that ran out of steps. (3) Multi-init hit-rate falls .494 -> .256 -> .009 across the ladder: at 50 clues the map is a GLOBAL attractor reachable from uniform-random states (genuine path-independence, EqR's claim, observed here without RI), and that property degrades with required depth.** **HONESTY / SCOPE: generator puzzles at a difficulty dial, NOT Sudoku-Extreme — nothing here may be compared to EqR's 99.8% @ 5.03M. n=1 seed, one arm; S-R2 (the RI dividend, the arm that decides whether EqR's biggest lever pays on a single-attractor landscape) awaits sudB. The 3-adic/dyadic risk registered before launch is NOT implicated at easy levels (97.5% solve) and remains a candidate contributor at 30 clues alongside depth.**

- **2026-08-14 (S-PORT CELL-1 LAUNCH REGISTRATION — H-33's decision rules locked BEFORE data; the port is BUILT and its instruments VERIFIED this entry: src/qhrrn2/sudoku.py (generator + uniqueness-preserving hole punch + episode adapter), one-task corpus builder, pretrain --sudoku, tools/probe_sudoku.py, 8 named tests, suite 105 green, end-to-end CPU smoke trains through the UNMODIFIED model)** — **WHY THE PORT IS CHEAP AND HONEST: the architecture needs ZERO edits (9x9 on the 32x32 canvas, one JIT shape) and three representation choices are symmetry claims with passing tests — digits 1-9 -> colors 1-9 so our exact S9 equivariance IS Sudoku's digit-relabeling symmetry (an inductive bias neither EqR nor FPRM carries; test verifies relabeled puzzles stay valid + uniquely solvable + blanks invariant); blank -> black(0), the palette fixed point; VOID kept distinct from blank. COMPARABILITY BY CONSTRUCTION: probe_sudoku IMPORTS ARC's own instrument functions (corrupt, rung_rng, retained, trace), same eps rungs, same 8 stab-steps — the only protocol difference is the one the domain forces (no keyhole TTT: the rule is universal, so the trained map + its single task row are used directly, which is also the EqR/FPRM no-TTT convention).** **CELL 1 (v5e-1 lane us-east1-c, ~$1 total, DIFFERENT quota family from the v6e rung-0 hunt so the two never contend): two arms, protocol-MATCHED to our ARC d16 line (d16/T6, 20k steps, anchor-p .3, knee beta 3e-5/1e-5, B64, seed 0, 8000 generated puzzles @ 30 givens, 64 held-out at a DIFFERENT eval seed): sudA = base, sudB = sudA + RI 0.15.** **DECISION RULES (locked): (S-R1, H-33-i "retention ~ solve") ARC's dissociation is retention 42/144 (29%) vs solve ~8/144 (5.5%), ratio ~5x. CONFIRM: R/S < 2 on the held-out 64. KILL: R/S >= 4 (the dissociation is architectural, not landscape-class). HONESTY GUARD (binding): if retention R < .15 the substrate simply did not learn Sudoku and the ratio is MEANINGLESS — the cell reports 'test void, substrate too weak' and buys a longer/deeper arm, never a confirmation. (S-R2, H-33-ii "RI pays here") sudB vs sudA solve rate on the SAME 64 puzzles, paired McNemar. CONFIRM: sudB > sudA, p<.05 or >=+5pp absolute. KILL: sudB <= sudA — RI's ARC null was then OURS (architecture/protocol), not a landscape-class fact, and H-33 loses its central mechanism. (S-R3, H-33-iii "basin-existence conditioning collapses") ARC measured 31x enrichment of capture on pairs holding a truth basin. Compute the same ratio here: multi-init hit rate on solve-FAILED puzzles, split by retention true/false. CONFIRM: enrichment < 3x. KILL: >= 10x. POWER GUARD: >=5 failed puzzles in each stratum or the readout is 'underpowered — reported, not concluded'. (S-R4, CROSS-DOMAIN LAW TESTS — the free riders that make this cell pay for itself twice) H-30: trained eta on a priced 20k Sudoku cell should land in the priced-20k band .14-.19 if the flow-velocity law is an ARCHITECTURE law; landing outside it says the band is ARC-landscape-specific. H-34: does a knee-profile-like spectrum appear on a NON-generative domain at all, and is Sudoku's profile the same universal shape? A different fixed profile would be the first evidence that the two-fixed-point structure is task-distribution-dependent. Both are reported descriptively (n=1 arm each) — no verdict from one cell.** **REGISTERED STRUCTURAL RISK (named before results, not after): Sudoku's constraint geometry is 3-ADIC (3x3 boxes, 9 = 3^2) while our RG pyramid is DYADIC (2x2 pooling) — boxes never align with pooling blocks at any scale. If the port underperforms, this is the first candidate cause (testable by box-aligned placement or a 3-adic pooling variant) and is itself a finding about whether RG coarse-graining must match a task's constraint arity. Second named risk: T6 may be far too shallow for constraint propagation (EqR unrolls 40k layers) — a weak substrate therefore triggers the T-scaling arm BEFORE any landscape-class conclusion is drawn.** **COMPARABILITY HONESTY (binding on the paper): these are GENERATED puzzles at a difficulty dial, NOT Sudoku-Extreme. No number from this cell may be set against EqR's 99.8% @ 5.03M unless their benchmark is vendored and run; ours are labeled 'generator Sudoku @ N givens'.**

RUNG 4 = d192 (6.87M, FPRM PARITY): winner ×3 on the decided steps branch, full battery + e1e3 + samp; if the quadratic branch fired at d96, this rung's scope is re-negotiated with the PI on the measured price (~$2.3k spot ×3 seeds) before launch. EVERY RUNG carries the free diagnostics: throat/S1 curve, knee-profile distance H-34, trained-η H-30, vacancy floor, Center H-35, per-family matrix, absolute S(ε) ladders — the paper's laws tested at every scale point, which is the discipline: no law is asserted at 7M that wasn't measured at 0.76/1.7/3/6.9M.** **COST/TIMELINE (spot v6e-8 ≈ $6.5/h; batteries +30–40%): rung 0 ≈ $60–100 · rung 1 ≈ $60–80 · rung 2 ≈ $280–350 · rung 3 ≈ $320–380 · rung 4 ≈ $550–650 (linear branch) — LADDER ≈ $1.3–1.55k; + convert/eval-6/ARC-1-eval + S-port ≈ $150–250; grand ≈ $1.5–1.8k against ≈$3.76k remaining (2× margin). Pod-days ≈ 12–14 sequential ⇒ ladder lands ~Sep 8–14 single-pod, ~Sep 1–5 with two pods or v6e-16 — the pending quota re-file (v6e 32 cores) is now MATERIALLY useful (halves rung-3/4 walls) and worth re-raising with the PI console. Freeze Sep 28 holds with paper-writing margin IF rungs stay gated and analysis rides between pod-days (the durability stack makes churny windows usable).** **AFTER THE LADDER: convert phase on the best substrate (keyhole+snap + S2-cell portfolio multi-init) → eval-6 dev-30 single shot (PI gate) → the FPRM head-to-head on ARC-1 public eval under the pre-registered matched-scoring protocol (2026-08-07 precedent) — the registered claim shape: parity-class substrate + the conversion/instrument stack as the strictly-additive differentiation, with the honest fallback claim being the efficiency curve (X% at 1.7–3M) if parity solve-rates fall short. S-port (H-33) runs parallel throughout on local + cheap lanes.**

Build: episode adapter (x = puzzle, y = solution; direct completion, no support/query, TTT-free eval per FPRM convention), generator corpus + the Sudoku-Extreme benchmark for comparability (EqR 99.8% @ 5.03M, TRM-lineage splits), d16-class pretrain first (small canvas → fast), then THE INSTRUMENT SUITE AS THE TEST SET: retention/ladders (does retention ≈ solve?), basin-existence conditioning (does the 31× ARC enrichment collapse toward 1?), RI arm (does the EqR dividend REAPPEAR?), multi-init coverage, barrier spectroscopy, spectral profiles (does a knee profile exist on a non-generative task family?). The result set either lands H-33 (the landscape-class law with cross-domain evidence — our instruments explaining EqR's own mechanism on their turf) or falsifies it informatively (architecture/protocol divergence). - **2026-08-14 (PHYSICS PASS over the wave-2 data, PI-directed — the banked results re-read through RG/holography/information/code-geometry lenses + adversarial pass + curiosity sweep; analyzer tools/analyze_p13w2_physics.py; artifact runs/analysis/p13w2_physics_20260814.txt; $0. THREE headline products: the knee profile passes its first out-of-sample test AND acquires its first mechanism that breaks it; the coupling mechanism story is REVISED by a code-level adversarial catch (gauge/renorm — this AMENDS the same-day verdict entry's mechanism, not its attribution); the flow-constant law is CORRECTED (eta tracks pricing+budget, not width))** — **(A) TWO-FIXED-POINT COLLAPSE, OUT-OF-SAMPLE PASS: all d64 arms + 80k + floors + NI collapse onto the knee profile (L1 dist .044-.090 vs free-profile .18-.25) — the cluster-O scaling function survives its first use as a diagnostic. THE ONE BREAK: Dri (RI-only) at d(knee)=.216 — profile [.592/.210/.102/.026/.070], UV share collapsed .69→.59 with mass rerouted to scale-1/2 (per-scale delta vs C53: −23.7 UV, +30.6 s1, +10.2 s2). RI training is the FIRST MECHANISM that moves the spectral fixed point: forcing convergence from arbitrary inits makes the code re-encode with mid-scale structure the VOID-start code never needed. Its family-level echo: Dri is the only wave-2 arm with HV 1/9 (others 4-6/9) — the axial/correspondence channel pays (the 08-13 HV-starvation hypothesis finds its real instance in RI, not scale; directional, n=9). (B) FLOORS ARE A SELF-CONSISTENT FIXED POINT: the normalized floor vector IS the knee profile (d=.048), and Dfloor sits at d=.044 — pricing AT the profile keeps the flow ON the profile. Per-scale: Dfloor's spectrum [342/64/47/13/30] sits AT-or-just-below its floors [350/75/50/15/30] — free-bits textbook at d64. NEW S2-FLAVORED DATUM (n=1, directional): C53's global-beta compresses ~54 nats BELOW the floor levels (443 vs Dfloor 497) and holds deep tail 17 vs Dfloor's 23 (best of all d64, = the d48 record) — first sighting of possible OVER-COMPRESSION damage: the nats below the measured floors may be load-bearing for deep basins. Registered watch: floors-vs-global at matched seed in pretrain-13-full decides it. (C) NI THERMAL TAX, LOCALIZED: B−C53 = +114 nats, UV-CONCENTRATED (+69.5 at s0, decaying up-scale) — state-space noise is finest-scale corruption and the code pays its Shannon rent exactly where the noise lives. (D) THE ADVERSARIAL CATCH THAT REVISES THE COUPLING MECHANISM: iterate_eq's coupled branch has NO renormalization (y_new = a1*y + a2*p, bare); the NI branch renormalizes as a SIDE EFFECT of its noise implementation (clip+renorm, training-only: rng=None at probes). Consequences, computed: (i) for B (trained WITH renorm), a1+a2 is a GAUGE DIRECTION — renormalized coupled update ≡ damped update with eta_eff = a2/(a1+a2) = .278, squarely in the C-band (.24-.29): B's 'expansive drift' is illusory, and 'NI counteracts the shedding' decomposes into noise + THE RENORM ITSELF — the protective agent is plausibly the restored simplex contract, not the noise (renorm-only arm = the one-flag decomposition cell, registered-unbuilt). (ii) For Dcoup (NO renorm anywhere), a1+a2=1.124 is PHYSICAL but BOUNDED: per-step mass m←a1*m+a2 has a stable fixed point at a2/(1−a1)=1.84; trained states reach m≈1.52 by T=6 while the retention probe injects truth at m=1.0 and extrapolates to m≈1.75 over its 8 stab-steps — THE RETENTION INSTRUMENT AND THE TRAINED DYNAMICS DISAGREE ABOUT THE STATE MANIFOLD on coupled-no-renorm arms, in both directions (late-step dynamics never trained at m=1; the probe horizon extends past the trained envelope). Dcoup's collapse-signature corroborates instrument-contract breakage over basin thermodynamics: its ladder is FLAT (15/16/15/15/16 across eps 0→.4 — eps-independent 'basins', i.e. the eps-axis stopped measuring), its within-envelope metrics are HEALTHY (exact@T 12 at T=6, ALL its exact pairs inside envelope), and B with the same scalars is immune. The 'expansive map sheds shallow basins' reading (pilot + this morning) is DEMOTED to unconfirmed; 'retention halving' for coupled arms is reclassified INSTRUMENT-CONFOUNDED. THE DESIGN CALL SHARPENS PAST 'coupling out as-parameterized': on simplex states the FPRM two-scalar form is either gauge-redundant (with renorm ⇒ ≡ eta) or contract-breaking (without) — there is NO useful version; drop it, keep eta. (FPRM's own states are unconstrained latents — the substrate-contract divergence explains why it works for them; related-work note.) (E) FLOW-CONSTANT LAW CORRECTED (26-ckpt table, section F): eta is ~WIDTH-INVARIANT at fixed price+budget — priced 20k arms sit at .14-.19 across d16-d48 (seeded d24 triple .140-.151 tight); PLAIN arms sit at .035-.10; the 40k/53k/80k cells jump to .24-.29 with C80 (.285) > 40k/53k (.24) and Dri at .323; B's gauge-corrected eta .278 joins the band. So the pilot's 'eta scales ~4x with d/T' is STRUCK — the .058 baseline was a PLAIN-arm number (pretrain8), and the real movers are PRICING (~3x), OPTIMIZATION BUDGET (+~60% at 40k+), and RI. Physics reading with the coarsening law: compressed, longer-optimized landscapes are coarser and support faster flow — the optimizer chooses its RG velocity to match basin scale. EqR's hand-set 0.05 matches our PLAIN regime — the convergence statement sharpened. (F) RI/EqR TRANSFER ADJUDICATED AT MECHANISM LEVEL (the multi-init deep-read): RI DID train what EqR says it trains — endpoint condensation is dramatic (nd=1 pairs 61-vs-35; the chaotic nd≥13 tail 4-vs-47 pairs) and where Dri condenses to ONE endpoint that endpoint is NEAR-TRUTH (med best_dist .108 vs C53's stuck-wrong .310) — genuine trained path-independence, not mode collapse. What did NOT transfer: the ACCURACY dividend — on det-failed pairs an alternative init reaches truth-radius at IDENTICAL rates (Dri 34/137 vs C53 36/134): the landscape INVENTORY is unchanged; RI reshapes convergence (how you arrive), not basin supply (what exists to arrive at). Why it works for EqR and not us: their tasks (Sudoku/Maze) are single-solution constraint-satisfaction — one attractor per instance, so path-independence IS accuracy; ARC truth-capture is inventory-limited (cluster-Q's 31x conditioning, fifth confirmation). RI also PAYS here: −19 retention, the spectral-profile break (A), and the HV echo. Their peer-reviewed +24pp is real physics for single-basin landscapes; on multi-basin generative landscapes the same mechanism buys convergence discipline that has nothing to decode into. (G) CURIOSITY SWEEP, remaining: (i) vacancy floor CONFIRMED at d64 — Copy + ExtractObjects 0/9 on ALL EIGHT p13 arms (now 0 on every substrate ever measured, 19 total); Count 0 on all six wave-2 arms — content-verbatim families are architecture-vacant at every scale tested (W-alpha strengthened). (ii) Center degrades with width (d48: 5/9 → d64 arms: 0-2/9) — a possible real width casualty, watch-item. (iii) Dcoup's family shedding is DIFFUSE except CompleteShape (5→0, the one family it zeroes; capture-class families survive) — noted for the shedding-vs-instrument disambiguation. (iv) life8 = 1.00 in every arm — retention is BINARY at 8 steps (idempotent-or-lost; no slow leaks): consistent with the horizon null, and it means retained_per_step carries no extra axis at this horizon. (v) Basin-edge fuzz (non-monotone ladder rows) = 6-14 pairs/arm (~4-10%) — quantified instrument noise at basin edges, part of the rg-radius volatility. (vi) Packing plane: rbar C53 .22 / C80 .23 / d48@40k .28 at matched N≈42/38 — NO d64 cell reaches the d48 frontier point; d64 is frontier-INTERIOR (the width-ceiling reading in the (N,r) plane). (vii) rg-vs-width dilution curve (seed-means): 16 / 11.7 / 11.7 / 17(T6) / 16 / 9 / 9.5 / 6 across d16→d64@80k with d24 seed-spread 6-16 — defensible statement only: no rise past d48, d64 at the bottom of the range, DEPTH (T6) again the count-friendly axis (Law-2 echo). STATISTICS: all new claims n=1-2 runs except the eta table (26 ckpts) and the vacancy floor (19 substrates); the coupling revision (D) is code-arithmetic, not statistics; spectral-profile claims use componentwise medians per the cluster-O convention.**

Three independent runs confirm the dynamics: the coupled pair drifts EXPANSIVE from contractive init in every arm that carries it (pilot A a1+a2=1.049, Dcoup 1.124, B 1.121) — FPRM's coupled residual scaling, on our anchor-trained substrate, is a marginal-basin shedder, and the pilot's 'radius record' decomposes as SURVIVOR BIAS (S(.2)/S(0) rises mechanically when the shallow shell sheds). Dri (RI-only) is a SECOND, milder shedder (ret 23, −19, p=.003, rad .87). Dfloor (floors-only) is CLEAN (−2, p=.856) and posts the best d64 deep tail (S.4 23 = the d48 record) — free-bits floors confirmed harmless-to-helpful. Direction favorable, tempered by the run-noise floor (10 counts, cross-run comparison); the registered wrong-stable half was NOT MEASURED (no e1e3 battery exists on any p13 arm — absence named); the spurious-attractor claim stays open. NI carries forward as an ARM, not a default.** **(4) RI MULTI-INIT (EqR's core claim): THE KILL FIRES-AS-FLAT — Dri coverage@16 59.7% vs C53 55.6% (+4.2pp, McNemar p=.18 n.s.); Dri's multi-init jump +2.8pp = exactly thermal-class (+2-3pp), nowhere near the single-bulk bar (65%). RI at ri_p=.15 does NOT buy truth-coverage. THE REFINEMENT THE KILL LANGUAGE MISSED: RI DID train path-independence — median n_distinct collapses 6.5→2 under Dri (random inits converge to FEWER endpoints, the trained init-invariance EqR promises) — but the consolidated endpoints are not truth more often. Init diversity was never the binding constraint; BASIN EXISTENCE is (cluster-Q's 31× conditioning, fourth confirmation). Combined with Dri's −19 retention: RI at .15 is NET-NEGATIVE for this stack as dosed.** **(5) TTT ADJUDICATION: THE PREDICTION FAILS (ewc ≥ kl ≥ plain is FALSE — KL is the WORST arm) and the kill does not fire (ewc 18 > plain 17, noise-range). NO anchor differentiates at λ=1.0: plain 17/11 (ret/exact@sel), kl 12/5, ewc 18/10 — vs keyhole 42/14 on the same substrate. KL@1.0 is the worst of both worlds (halves exact 11→5 while protecting nothing); EWC@1.0 is a no-op vs plain (pairwise flips 0-vs-1). Pretrains are ckpt-resumable -> running 1-chip arms can MIGRATE to the pod mid-run if the bump clears.** Campaign state at entry: lane tpu = 32c-s1 chain training; lanes b/c = d48c/d48p relaunching with remat; lane d = T6 prep.

At d16 pricing COSTS retention count (49->44); at d32 it ADDS (38->47). The priced-minus-plain contrast GROWS with capacity — S2's excess-flux-is-memorization, now measured as an interaction: unpriced capacity overfits the seen instance distribution (its excess flux visibly spent, its basins instance-tuned); priced capacity converts to radius.** FINAL P1-P4: **P1 load-bearing half CONFIRMED (priced throat 785/663/639 — a task-set constant across 78k->220k params); the free-grows half holds only as a weak d32 uptick coincident with the transfer collapse — consistent with S2 but not independently significant. Area-law decay scale-robust (I_0/I_4: 11-21x priced, 157-241x free). Operating point COMMITS only if seed 1 reproduces the (radius, exact, tail) leadership; then convert phase: B-TTT lane cell + PoE + populations on the d32-priced pair, pretrain-12 arms finalized from the interaction (priced-only at d>=24; curriculum axis from P4).**

- **2026-08-11 (T3 SCALE SWEEP — PARTIAL VERDICT, 5 of 6 cells (d32-plain battery still in flight): P1 SPLITS and the refuted half is the BETTER result — flux is a TASK property, not a capacity property; CAPACITY BUYS CODE DISTANCE, NOT BANDWIDTH; P3 (retention grows with d) is NULL; P2 (radius grows with d) SUPPORTED on all three task sets)** — Cells: d in {16,24,32} x {plain, priced-knee}, C20 corpus, 20k steps, seed 0; batteries = ladder + sample x {val-hard, rg-48, rt-48}; d16 = P10-A/C. BUT the free arms are ALSO d-invariant (157,558 (d16) / 156,170 (d24); per-scale UV cut 115,869 vs 118,237) — the registered 'free flux grows with d' half is REFUTED. SHARPENED S1/S2 STATEMENT (memo-grade, and stronger than the original prediction): TOTAL INFORMATION FLUX IS SET BY THE TASK DISTRIBUTION AND THE PRICE, NOT BY MODEL CAPACITY. Extra width does not buy extra bandwidth and does not spend it either. Geometric per-scale decay is preserved under pricing at every d (I_0/I_4 = 11-21x priced; 157-241x free), so the operational area law is scale-robust.** **P2 — SUPPORTED, and cleanest exactly where it matters (transfer): radius S(.2)/S(0) on val-hard .55 (d16c) / .54 (d24c) / .70 (d32c); on rg-48 (unseen families) .12 / .23 / .40 — MONOTONE in d; on rt-48 (trained families) .11 (d24c) / .39 (d32c). d32-priced also posts the sweep's best val-hard exact@T (12) and the widest tail (S(.4)=24 vs 14-20 elsewhere).** **P3 — NULL: retention COUNT S(0) does not grow with d (val-hard plain 49->51; priced 44->50->47; every paired McNemar p in [.31,.87], no cell significant). Combined with P2 this is the SAME COUNT-vs-RADIUS DISSOCIATION found for dials-vs-pricing (2026-08-10 EOS entry), now along the capacity axis: WIDTH BUYS BASIN RADIUS (code distance), NOT BASIN COUNT (codebook size). So family novelty DOES bind. BUT both sit far below val-hard (47-51): the dominant term is generator-INSTANCE hardness, not family novelty (RE-ARC samples a much wider instance distribution per family than the fixed originals). BOTH branches of the registered decision are therefore live, with magnitudes: instance hardness ~3x, family novelty ~1.6-1.8x. Consequence: curriculum/difficulty-scheduling and capacity BOTH earn a place in pretrain-12; the C21 compositional sampler is NOT yet needed (novelty is not the dominant wall).** **STATISTICS, STATED HONESTLY: no single paired contrast reaches significance at n=144; the P2 claim rests on consistent SIGN STRUCTURE across three independent task sets plus the exact@T and tail corroboration, at n=1 seed per cell. The registered seed-replication rule therefore BINDS before any operating-point commitment. Provisional operating point pending d32-plain + replication: d32-priced (radius + exact + tail), with d24-priced the coverage-optimal alternative (cov 92).**
