- **2026-08-11 (PRETRAIN-10 CAMPAIGN VERDICTS Q1-Q4: the DOSE KILL FIRES (beta saturates at the knee — B1-full redesigns to per-scale/free-bits pricing); the corpus bet PAYS in-distribution (+4-5 retention); TRUE family transfer is NEAR-FLOOR with one missing attribution cell registered; ZERO TPUs verified; campaign ≈ $9)** — Four arms trained to 20k on the C20 corpus (identical 1803 tasks / 12,619 pairs, laws enforced; final val_pix A .687 / B .684 / C .652 / D .620 — the broader corpus fits harder than pretrain-9's .757, and fit != landscape, see Q3), full battery grid (probe_ladder + probe_sample x val-hard + rg-48 per arm; runs/analysis/p10_battery_20260811.txt; all cells 48/48, sentinels clean). **Q1 DOSE-RESPONSE — THE REGISTERED KILL FIRES: P10-D (beta 1e-4) is NOT above P10-C (3e-5) on radius (S.2/S0 .48 vs .55) NOR hops (2 vs 6) — the monotone dose prediction is DEAD. The throat itself DOES dose-respond (I_med 785 -> 380 nats; fitting does not break — anchors soften the knee found in from-scratch fits) but the GEOMETRY stops paying past the knee: compression and basin quality decouple at high beta. Registered consequence executes: B1-full's design pivots from global-beta dose to per-scale/free-bits pricing (price the cuts that carry excess, floor the cuts that carry structure). Replication half: C posts the best radius (.55) and best hops (6, vs p9c's 5) BUT plain-s0 posts .53 — the seed spread (.42-.53) is wider than the priced-vs-plain gap on this corpus; the width claim stands on the JOINT pattern (C best on radius AND hops AND the only arm above baseline on rg) and is explicitly seed-noise-limited at n=2 seeds. NEW NUANCE: D posts the highest sampled coverage (91/144) with the fewest exact hops — a narrow throat spreads samples without snapping them; hop quality, not coverage, is the width signal.** **Q3 CORPUS EFFECT — PAYS: plain arms 49/48 val-hard retention vs pretrain-9 plain 43/45 (+4-5 at identical arch/width/steps; the generator corpus improves the landscape even on the OLD task distribution while fitting worse — fit-vs-landscape dissociation, third sighting).** **Q2 TRUE FAMILY TRANSFER — NEAR-FLOOR, with the priced arm the only one above baseline: rg-48 retention A 13 / B 12 / C 16 / D 8 vs p8-baseline 13 (which had most gate ORIGINALS in-corpus); exact hops 0 across ALL arms on unseen families. Honest read: at d16/20k, training 271 generator families lifts unseen-family retention barely-to-not-at-all for plain arms; C's +3 is noise-range. CONFOUND REGISTERED (the missing attribution cell, queued): no arm was measured on FRESH INSTANCES OF ITS OWN 271 TRAINED FAMILIES — without that cell, 'family transfer absent' vs 'RE-ARC instances are hard for d16 at any familiarity' is UNDECIDED. rg-train48 cell (one lane-hour): seeded 48 train-families x fresh instances, probe_ladder, all four arms; decision: trained-family retention >> 13-16 => transfer-specific failure (family novelty binds — C21 compositional sampler and/or capacity d24+ become the levers); trained-family ~ gate => instance hardness/fit-limit binds (keyhole fit-side work or difficulty curriculum).** **Q4 NL-PROTECTION: DEFERRED honestly — requires family-type labels for the rg set; not evaluable from this battery alone; queued alongside the rg-train48 cell.** Portfolio note: pretrain-10R (repulsion) remains registered-unbuilt; its case is STRENGTHENED by Q2 (if family novelty binds, complementary codes are the near-term private-set lever at fixed capacity). Campaign totals: 4 pretrains + 4 batteries + 2 rg-baselines ≈ $9; every arm canary-gated; zero TPUs verified at close.

- **2026-08-10 (rg-BASELINE VERDICT: the gate is HARD, not easy — current substrates hold almost no basins for generator-family instances; the corpus hole is measured at 3x retention deficit; width does NOT transfer across this distribution shift)** — probe_ladder x rg gate-48 x {p8, p9c} (2 lanes, canary-gated, rescued, torn down; runs/rgbase_p8, runs/rgbase_p9c). **p8: exact 0/144, GT-retention 13/144 (9.0%) vs its 42/144 (29.2%) on val-hard — a 3x deficit; only 7/48 gate families show ANY retention (2 fully, 5 partial); ladder near-empty (7/2/1/0). p9c: exact 1/144, retention 9/144 (6.2%), same 7/48 concentration — the priced substrate's val-hard width advantage does NOT survive this distribution shift (13-vs-9 is noise-range; both near-floor).** Registered C20 risk (too-easy gate) is DEAD — the instrument has wide dynamic range in the useful direction. READINGS: (1) even though p8/p9c pretrained on most gate families' ORIGINALS, instance-level generalization within known families is weak — basins are tuned to the seen instance distribution, not to family rules as such (the honest scope of everything measured on val-hard); (2) the generator corpus is not merely more data — it closes a MEASURED hole (this baseline is the pre-treatment number for pretrain-10's Q2/Q3); (3) fits are mechanically healthy on rg tasks (wall p50 12s) — the deficit is representational, not infrastructural. Caveat registered: e_t-only keyhole fits may under-fit RE-ARC's harder supports — fit-side vs basin-side decomposition available later via full-fit rows if Q2 needs it.

- **2026-08-10 (PRETRAIN-10 PRE-RUN REGISTRATION + rg-BASELINE BATTERY — the private-set campaign; every arm carries a same-day measurement justifying it)** — **SEQUENCE: (i) rg-baseline battery FIRST (2 lanes, ~30 min): probe_ladder on rg_ gate-48 x {p8, p9c} — calibrates gate difficulty (registered C20 risk: too-easy gate = insensitive instrument) and records the INSTANCE-generalization baseline for current substrates (scope note in the C20 entry binds: these are not family-transfer numbers). (ii) PRETRAIN-10, four arms, four lanes, overnight.** **CORPUS (identical across arms, laws enforced in-code, counts printed at launch): ARC-train originals minus dev-30 minus gate-100 (~270 tasks, orbit x4) + RE-ARC train-family instances (271 families x 20 verified, rearc_seed 0, separate re_ rows, no orbit) + ConceptARC minus val-hard-48 (as pretrain-8/9). ARMS (d16, equilibrium, anchor-p 0.3 eps 0.15, 20k steps, K=64, T=4 — the pretrain-9 base; dials parked): P10-A seed 0 plain; P10-B seed 1 plain (seed noise floor + portfolio); P10-C beta_flux=3e-5, beta_nl=1e-5 (P9-C replication on the generator corpus — does the width result transfer across corpora?); P10-D beta_flux=1e-4, beta_nl=1e-5 (THE DOSE ARM — past the measured from-scratch binding-family knee (3e-5,1e-4); risk registered: pricing may break fitting at this dose at corpus scale; anchors may shift the knee — that IS the question).** **REGISTERED QUESTIONS + DECISION RULES: (Q1, dose-response, THE registered prediction from cluster M) exact-hop rate under probe_sample and basin radius S(.2)/S(0) under probe_ladder rise monotonically A/B -> C -> D; kill for the width-scaling bet: D <= C on both radius and hop rate (beta saturates at the knee — B1-full redesigns around per-scale/free-bits pricing instead of global dose). (Q2, family transfer) probe_ladder retention + coverage on rg_ gate-48 (TRUE family-transfer, laws enforced): priced arms >= plain arms transfers the width advantage to UNSEEN families — the private-set forecast number; kill: priced retention collapses on unseen families (width was family-specific memorization geometry). (Q3, corpus effect) all-arm val-hard instrument battery vs pretrain-9 rows (bigger/broader corpus at fixed width: retention up or down?). (Q4, NL protection) rg-gate families stratified by RE-ARC family type where inferable: priced arms must not crater correspondence-class families vs plain (the attention-closure risk named in the audit; if they do, beta_nl gets a free-bits floor in B1-full). BATTERIES (after all four arms land): probe_ladder + probe_sample on val-hard AND rg_ gate-48, all four substrates — one lane-day total. REPULSION (pretrain-10R) registered-unbuilt: brief fine-tune of P10-A vs P10-B against each other's frozen errors (cluster I; target = Center2-p1-class coverage); design lands only after A/B batteries exist.** Cost estimate: 4 lanes x ~2-3h pretrain + ~1 lane-day batteries ≈ $15-20. All launches canary-gated; heartbeat armed; teardown per lane on completion.

- **2026-08-10 (C20 COMPLETE: RE-ARC corpus integration + FROZEN family-transfer gate; two contamination laws found and closed at build time; suite 78 green)** — **C20a corpus mixing shipped**: build_corpus(rearc_families, per_family, seed) — each family = its OWN `re_<fam>` row (NOT merged with the base task's row: RE-ARC generators occasionally broaden the original rule — sharing a row would blur two nearby programs into one embedding; separate rows also give clean ablation), no orbit expansion on re_ rows (the generator IS the diversity source), C16 contract otherwise unchanged. pretrain.py --rearc plumbs it. **TWO CONTAMINATION LAWS caught by the careful pass and now enforced in code (tools/pretrain.py prints the measured counts at every launch): (law 1) dev-30 families NEVER enter as generator instances (RE-ARC covers all 400 training tasks incl. dev-30's — 29 of dev-30's 30 landed in the train-300 split and are removed; 1 sits in gate-100 where it never pretrains anyway); (law 2) gate-family ORIGINALS are excluded from the pretraining corpus (they are training-split tasks currently in-corpus — without this the family gate measures instance-generalization, not family-transfer). Measured law line at smoke: 271 RE-ARC train families in, 100 gate originals out.** **C20c gate FROZEN: rg_ gate-48 = 48 seeded families from gate-100 minus dev-30, 3 support + 3 query verified instances each, difficulty uniform(0,1), builder deterministic (tools/build_regate.py, seed 20260810) — REBUILD REPRODUCES BIT-EXACT, committed checksum tools/re_gate48.sha256 = 75dfeba5... (the JSONs live under git-ignored data/re_gate48; the sha makes any regeneration verifiable). rg_ loader route in grid.load_task.** Named tests: 6 in tests/test_rearc.py (families load; split deterministic/disjoint; episode contract; difficulty dial moves complexity — verified live; gate loader route; corpus mixing + no-orbit-on-re-rows). Full suite 78 green. Pretrain smoke (30 steps, d8, 5-base + 271-family corpus): loss descends, checkpoints round-trip. IMPORTANT SCOPE NOTE for the paper: for CURRENT substrates (p8/p9*) the rg_ gate measures INSTANCE-generalization only (their corpora contained most gate-family originals); TRUE family-transfer numbers exist only for pretrain-10+ whose corpus obeys law 2.

- **2026-08-10 (STEP-2 REGISTRATION: generator corpus + FAMILY-HOLDOUT LAW — the private-set instrument; C20)** — Design decision (analysis before code, PI's known-ARC-overfit constraint binding): RE-ARC-class generators (per-task parameterized samplers, Hodel, public) are vendored for immediate corpus breadth; their known-family bias is CONVERTED INTO AN INSTRUMENT by a **family-level holdout law: the pretrain-10 bulk trains on a seeded ~75% of generator families; the remaining ~25% of FAMILIES (never seen as a family, not just as instances) form the resamplable family-transfer gate — unseen-rule-family generalization measured directly, unlimited fresh instances, zero evidentiary decay. All steering moves to this gate; curated sets (dev-30, ARC eval) reserved for final claims under the existing holdout law.** Components: C20a vendor RE-ARC + adapter to our episode format (validation: rendered samples + loader CI + train smoke); C20b family-holdout split (seeded, recorded); C20c the family-transfer battery = the standard instruments (retention, eps-ladders, exact-hop rate, coverage) run on held-out-family tasks — the substrate-level private-set forecast. Compositional rule-DSL sampler (novel families beyond ARC's) = registered follow-up C21, unbuilt, triggered if family-transfer is strong within RE-ARC families but the gap to curated-hard says family NOVELTY (not diversity) binds. Kill/risk named: RE-ARC distribution too easy/regular (generator tasks solved trivially at high rates would make the gate insensitive — check instance difficulty distribution at validation).

- **2026-08-10 (CLUSTER-M VERDICT: temperature does NOT replace weight diversity — cluster I confirmed as a pretrain-10 arm; but width converts temperature into a DECODER — the exact-hop rate is the self-decoding embryo and pretrain-10's dose-response prediction; ZERO TPUs verified)** — 48 val-hard tasks x k=16 x T0 {0.1,0.4} x {p8, p9c} (lanes canary-gated, rescued, torn down; runs/samp_p8, runs/samp_p9c). **PRIMARY (registered bars): coverage@16 p8 55.6%/54.9%, p9c 57.6%/56.2% — BOTH substrates below the single-bulk bar (~65%) and far below the 32-member pool (72%); deterministic points already give 53.5%/54.2%, so 16 thermal samples of one weight configuration buy only +2-3.4pp. Rule-3 second clause FIRES: diversity must come from WEIGHTS — replica repulsion (cluster I) is CONFIRMED as a pretrain-10 arm. Third independent confirmation of the portfolio law (candidates, retention-unions, now sampling), and the cleanest: same-weight temperature exploration measurably cannot substitute for cross-weight code diversity (replica-wormhole reading: samples explore ONE watershed structure).** NOT the kill: the sampler is alive (p8 median 6-9 distinct endpoints vs 1 deterministic) and produces real basin-hops — 2-5 EXACT truth-captures per arm on det-failed pairs (incl. Center2-q1, the retirement-blocker pair, reached natively in the smoke). **WIDTH-INTERACTION (registered secondary): resolves BOTH WAYS — p9c (priced, widest basins) COLLAPSES thermal diversity (median 2 distinct vs p8's 6-9: wide basins swallow the walk) yet DOUBLES exact truth-captures (5 vs 3): width concentrates sampling onto the code's actual codewords — low-entropy, high-quality; temperature becomes a self-decoder on the priced landscape. EXACT-HOP RATE (~3.5% of pairs at k=16, current beta) = the self-decoding embryo; REGISTERED DOSE-RESPONSE PREDICTION for pretrain-10's battery: exact-hop rate rises with beta (wider basins -> more truth-capture under thermal sampling). - **2026-08-10 (CLUSTER-M DESIGN AMENDMENT before campaign — the smoke reproduced [R-4] exactly: logit-noise at eta=0.058 is a silently-dead sampler; corrected to state-space Langevin at the canonical scale)** — 4-sample smoke on ca_Center2/p8: n_distinct 1/4 at BOTH sigma_0 in {0.5, 2.0}, every query — zero endpoint diversity. Mechanism: noise injected on LOGITS is damped by the learned eta (y <- y + 0.058*(p_noisy - y): ~x17 attenuation per step), the linear anneal reaches sigma=0 at the final step, and the restoration-trained basins relax the residue — the quench stayed effectively zero-temperature. **[R-4] ("mis-scaled Langevin noise/trigger implementations fail silently", refuted register, July) predicted this failure class; the register did its job on our own build. CORRECTED DESIGN (supersedes the sigma-design in the registration below): y_{t+1} = y + eta*(p(y) - y) + sqrt(2*T_t*eta)*xi, xi Gaussian per cell/channel mean-subtracted per cell (simplex-tangent), y clipped+renormalized; T_t = T0 for t < t_anneal(=12), 0 after (quench phase lets basins capture); t_total 16; T0 grid {0.1, 0.4}; k=16.** All other registration terms (coverage-only instruments, comparators, decision rules, kill) unchanged.

Solve union stable at 2/30 across eval-2/3/4 under pretrain-2; bulk-seed sensitivity (d511f180/9565186b solved under pretrain-1 only) remains the other open thread → pretrain-3 seed study and the d631b094 sel-trace probe queued alongside eval-5.** Spend today ≈ $3; cumulative ≈ $58. + day plan: pretrain-2, val-20 TTT ablation, eval-2 — registrations before any result)** — **C1-v3, content-conditioned size head (mixture over measured candidates).** Eval-1's named failure: the v2 relative frame cannot express size = f(content) (counting val_pix 1.00 / exact=False in all four arms; crop-to-smallest 0.00). v3 generalizes v2's own extrapolation logic: **p(out_size = s) = Σ_c q_c · p_off(s − cand_c + 15)** — a selection distribution q over K=8 MEASURED candidates per axis [in_ax, in_other, 2·in_ax, 3·in_ax, ⌈in_ax/2⌉, ⌈in_ax/3⌉, occupied-lines(ax), top-color-count], each observable from the input at predict time (no GT), with the EXISTING 30-way offset head applied relative to the selected candidate. v2 is the special case q = δ(cand = in_ax) — selection-over-measurements extrapolates by construction, exactly as the offset frame did for same-size. Cost ≈ +1k params (hc→2×8 selection logits). Named tests (CI-6 extension, shipped with the build): (t1) construction-expressibility — same-size, ×2, ×3, transpose, and count targets each reachable exactly by some (q, offset) at init-scale params; (t2) **count extrapolation** — synthetic size-only fit where out_w = colored-cell count, validated on UNSEEN counts (the trap v2's absolute classes failed); (t3) v2-regression — extent extrapolation (CI-6's (9,6) case) still passes via candidate 1. **Day plan (in order): (1) build C1-v3 + suite green locally; (2) pretrain-2** — identical registered config to pretrain-1 EXCEPT the head (head-only change for clean attribution; orbit expansion stays parked for pretrain-3); **(3) TTT-protocol ablation on CORPUS val-20 only** (holdout-safe): arms {A, B} × steps {600, 2000} × β_TTT {0, 1e-5} at fixed registered lr's, scored by val-20 exact solves then val_pix; **(4) eval-2 pre-registration**: dev-30, all four arms at the val-20-winning protocol (D at the same step budget = matched null), pass@2, ALL-test-pairs exactness, same seed policy; registered prediction: counting converts (candidate 8 ≡ the rule), fractal/mirror aided (candidates 3/4), crop-to-smallest NOT claimed (content-relocation gap, not size); expected band 3–8/30. Results + critique appended when they exist. **SAME-DAY APPEND (pretrain-2 + val-20 ablation results; eval-2 protocol LOCKED before it runs):** pretrain-2 (C1-v3 head, 77,977 params) completed 20k steps, val-20 raw-predict pix plateau ~0.73 (vs 0.76–0.78 for pretrain-1 — head change shifts the loss balance; noted, not alarming; decisive data = TTT). **Val-20 TTT ablation (160 fits): ZERO pass@2 solves in all 8 cells** — the fit-budget lever does NOT unlock exactness (600→2000: A +0.03 val_pix to 0.800, B +0.005; LoO-exact stays 0–2/20); **β=1e-5 at TTT consistently HURTS** (−0.02 to −0.05 in all four paired comparisons) — lever (iii) closed at this β; eval-1 sub-cause (c) (budget binds) demoted: more steps alone is not the blocker. **Eval-2 lock: dev-30, arms A/B/C/D, steps=2000, β_TTT=0, seed 0, pass@2, all-test-pairs exactness, overnight detached run (wall 28800s).** What eval-2 uniquely tests: C1-v3's effect on the size-gap families (counting, mirror-extend, fractal) through pretrain-2 — the registered 3–8/30 band stands, now resting almost entirely on the size-head fix. — first real-task solve rates; same-day as registration, arms locked before results)** — **Pretrain-1**: 20k steps in 28 min wall on v5e-1 spot (13.1 it/s warm), loss 4.70→~1.0; val-20 pix 0.680→plateau 0.76–0.78 from ~10k (exact ≤1/20 throughout — exactness, not learning, is the bottleneck); NOTE: A_total peaked ~1.2e6 nats then **declined unpriced** to ~0.77e6 — the optimizer sheds attention transport even at β=0 (C14 watch-item, favorable). **Eval-1** (120 cells, ~50 min TPU): **pass@2 — A 1/30, B 1/30, C 0/30, D 0/30; union 2/30.** Solves: d511f180 (color 5↔8 swap) by **arm A — 32 fitted parameters**, val_pix 1.00; 445eab21 (select-larger-rect argmax) by arm B. Both at attempt 1. **Registered comparison 1 (H-14 kill test): NOT killed** — C beats D on LoO val_pix 20 wins / 5 losses / 5 ties (sign test p≈0.004), means 0.759 vs 0.683; transfer is real but modest and buys zero extra exact solves at this scale. **Registered comparison 2 (boundary sufficiency): SUPPORTED** — A mean 0.792, B 0.794 vs C 0.759 (A beats C 14/10); both solves came from boundary arms, full fine-tuning solved nothing. **S2 datum (first real-task flux dataset, runs/eval1/results.jsonl)**: mean A_tot at selected checkpoints — **D ≈ 9.6e11 nats** (from-scratch full fit routes everything through the free attention channel — the unpriced-loophole pathology in the wild), **C ≈ 3.4e6** (pretrained init: ~5 orders more compressed), **A/B ≈ 1.4–1.6e6**. Boundary restriction + pretraining ⇒ ~6 orders of magnitude information-cost reduction at equal-or-better accuracy. Family-prediction check: WRONG on geometry (predicted-easy D4/mirror unsolved; rot180 max 0.67) — actual solves were color-map + argmax; R2 tasks unsolved as predicted but with HIGH val_pix (0.94–0.96). **Critical analysis (companion practice).** *Mechanism of the exactness gap (central finding)*: median val_pix ~0.85, solves ~0; named sub-causes: (a) **size-head structural gap** — counting d631b094 has val_pix 1.00 in ALL arms with exact=False: content perfect, size wrong; the relative-offset frame cannot express size = f(content) (bar length = object count); same class kills 23b5c85d (crop-to-SMALLEST: 0.00 everywhere) and hurts fractal ×3; **C1-v3 candidate registered: content-conditioned size head**; (b) last-mile interior pixel noise on 0.90–0.96 cells (the constructed-program translate story recurring on real tasks); (c) **fit budget binds**: LoO validation reached exactness in 1/120 cells within 600 steps — real tasks fit slower than constructed families at the same budget. *Trustworthiness*: n=30, one seed, one TTT budget, LoO val_pix is a single-pair metric; C-vs-D direction consistent; solve counts too small for family claims; **no protocol tuning touched dev-30 — the holdout is clean for eval-2**. *Levers (all corpus-side, holdout-safe, in priority order)*: (i) TTT-budget/lr ablation on CORPUS tasks (val-20), then ONE dev-30 re-eval with the chosen budget = eval-2; (ii) size-head v3; (iii) β>0 at TTT time (D's blowup suggests pricing-as-regularizer may aid exactness); (iv) pretrain-2 with orbit expansion. *Infra lessons (all fixed + committed same day)*: macOS bsdtar mac-metadata extracts as ._ files under GNU tar (3-layer fix); save_ckpt scalar mangling broke Config hashability (fix + type-survival test); checkpoint-consuming paths must be smoke-tested with a REAL checkpoint locally before TPU spend. Spend today ≈ $2–3; total ≈ $52/$2000.
- **2026-08-01 (C16 registered; dev-30 FROZEN; pretrain-1 + eval-1 PRE-REGISTERED — all before any joint-training result exists)** — Solve-rate sprint, build leg. **(a) Dev-30 frozen at 30/30** (commit 10c3ac9; 20 families, 10 paired, R1-type 10/30, R2 3/30 + 2 correspondence-adjacent; verified spares ea32f347, 74dd1130 recorded with exclusion reasons). **Corpus law: pretraining corpus = ARC-1 public training split (400) minus dev-30 = 370 tasks, all their pairs (support + query); dev-30 pairs NEVER enter pretraining — support pairs are seen only at TTT time, queries only at scoring.** This is the *inductive* protocol — deliberately stricter than mdlARC's transductive joint-TTT; a transductive arm may be added later as a separate registered run, never silently. **(b) C16 design.** Per-task embedding table E ∈ R^(N_tasks×32) (370×32 ≈ 11.8k params, pretraining scaffolding — discarded at eval like optimizer state; eval-time cost is ONE e_t = 32 params/task). Entry points, exactly two: (i) rule-path bias h_ir ← h_ir + W_e·e_t (W_e: 32→d_ir, shared) — S9-safe (task vector is color-blind, equivariance preserved; test extends CI-1 suite); (ii) per-task color bias: color_bias_t = reshape(W_cb·e_t, (11, d)) added to the shared Amendment-A bias in embed and decoder init — **[P-C2]-grade necessity argument: h_ir is S9-INVARIANT by construction (symmetric-mean pooling), so the rule channel provably cannot carry task-specific color constants; without (ii), every color-constant rule in a 370-task corpus is unrepresentable under a shared bulk** — the same contradiction that forced Amendment A, now at corpus scale. e_t = 0 ⇒ bit-exact backward compatibility (CI-7 test; all prior gates/checkpoints unaffected). **(c) Augmentation validity law honored at corpus scale:** a D4/palette-transformed episode NEVER shares the base task's embedding (that is exactly the contradictory-supervision trap of 2026-07-20); if used, transformed episodes are *virtual tasks* with their own rows. Pretrain-1 runs n_orbit=1 (identity only) + placement offsets (always rule-safe); orbit expansion is the registered run-2 data lever. **(d) Pretrain-1 config (registered before launch):** d=16, K=64, M=2, T=4, τ=1.0, attn_max_hw=32; B=64 pairs/step, task-balanced sampling (uniform over tasks, then over the task's pairs); AdamW lr 1e-3, cosine to 3e-5, 500-step warmup, wd 1e-4, grad-clip 1.0; 20k steps, checkpoint + metrics every 1k; **β_flux = β_nl = 0 with BOTH flux ledgers logged at every checkpoint** — registered risk: the measured free-attention blowup (A→9.3e7 nats, C14 probe); kill condition: if pretrain-1 shows A_s ≥ 1e7-scale AND dev-30 transfer fails (arm C ≈ arm D), run-2 is a β-warmup pretrain, not more β=0 epochs. Held-in signal: **val-20** — 20 seeded-random corpus tasks whose QUERY pairs are excluded from the pair pool and scored (exact-match via predict(), trained e_t) every 1k steps = within-task generalization monitor (R1 early warning), costs 20 training pairs. **(e) Eval-1 pre-registration (dev-30; run after pretrain-1; no peeking before arms are locked — this entry is the lock):** per task, TTT arms: **A** = fit e_t only (32 params; fresh e_t initialized to the table mean = empirical program prior); **B** = e_t + color_bias (+role_emb excluded) ≈ 208 params at d=16; **C** = full fine-tune from the pretrained checkpoint (ceiling); **D** = full fit from random init (the null: today's CI-3a protocol, pretraining adds nothing). All arms: identity transform + placement offsets only (admissible orbit is UNKNOWN on real tasks — no orbit augmentation, no orbit voting), LoO/MDL selection exactly as shipped in `fit_loo`, 600 steps, lr 3e-3 for C/D and 1e-2 for A/B (few-param arms need larger steps; any lr tuning happens on corpus tasks only, never on dev-30). **Pass@2 = {LoO-selected checkpoint prediction, final-params prediction}; solve = exact match on ALL test pairs of the task (ARC rule); headline metric = solves/30 per arm.** Registered comparisons, in order: (1) C vs D — does joint pretraining transfer at all (the H-14 kill test); (2) A,B vs C — boundary-sufficiency (the C10/C12 philosophy bet); (3) per-family stratification — registered prediction: transfer most likely on families with corpus twins (translation, color-mapping, D4/rot, mirror-extend), least on R2/NL (3631a71a, 29ec7d0e, 496994bd). S2 payload logged per arm×task: I_s/A_s spectra at the selected checkpoint, LoO val curves, steps-to-first-val-exact — the first real-task S2 dataset, riding the solve-rate leg as decided 2026-07-30. Build lands as `src/qhrrn2/episodic.py` + `tools/pretrain.py` + `tools/eval_dev30.py` + CI-7 tests; measured d=16 param count appended to this entry at build completion. **BUILD COMPLETE same day: CI-7 GREEN (8 new tests; suite 33/33). Measured params at d=16: bulk 76,937 (incl. 6,864 task_proj: e_ir 32→32, e_cb 32→176 @ scale 1e-2) — 5.2× under the 400k ceiling, 91× under TRM; table 370×32 = 11,840 (scaffolding). CPU smoke (8 tasks, d=8, 40 steps): loss 5.31→3.0, checkpoints + resume verified; the free-attention monitor already shows A_total rising at smoke scale — the registered risk is visible and watched, as designed.**

Ironic instance of our own thesis: the optimizer took transport over computation. **Binding decisions:** (1) the constructed-family measurement program is **CLOSED v1** — the frozen dataset (envelopes, floors, class ablation, 34-row F1 partial) is the paper's toy-scale S1/S3 material; F1's missing cells and further sweeps are September-if-time items; (2) **C15 parked** as registered-unimplemented until real NL tasks demonstrate the attention channel matters at all; (3) **this week is the solve-rate sprint**: dev-30 → 30, joint-episodic trainer (shared bulk + per-task embedding table ≈ our boundary-param philosophy applied at corpus scale; mdlARC-informed), first d=16 pretrain on TPU, dev-30 eval — first real solve-rate number AND first S2 flux/LoO-gap data on real tasks from the same run. S2 instrumentation rides the solve-rate leg; the measurement discipline serves the project, not itself.

F3's "binding fragility" reinterpreted: binding through the redundant two-channel system is fragile; the minimal architecture binds robustly at 30× harsher prices — an architecture-property, not a task-property. **(2) Envelopes collapse again**: checkerboard 268 (23× below the with-attn 6,237; ~67× above its ~4-nat floor), colorswap 539 (23×), **constfill 64 vs ~2-nat reference — the closest sandwich yet (~30×)**. - **2026-07-30 (class ablation)** — **Attention-absent ablation 10/10 EXACT (β=1e-5, 2 seeds × 5 families; teardown verified, zero TPUs): all five constructed families are locality class L**, matching their construction — the class instrument agrees with ground truth. Three results: **(1) Envelopes tighten 4–9× without the attention channel** (identity 1,124 vs 5,617; checkerboard 809 vs 6,237; translate 1,475 vs 13,247; colorswap 2,370 vs 12,438; constfill ~equal) — the priced attention channel pays substitution rent on local tasks even under pricing; identity's sandwich gap narrows to **11×** (1,124 vs 103-nat reference). **(2) Translate becomes RELIABLE: 2/2 exact vs 3/8 with attention at the same β** — the nonlocal channel was destabilizing a local task. **(3) First architecture-selection datum for S3: for class-L tasks the minimal (attention-free) architecture is strictly better — tighter envelopes AND higher reliability, measured.** Frontier protocol v3 (binding): envelopes are minima over accuracy-qualified rows across β AND architecture variants. New attn-absent envelope ordering: constfill 422 < checkerboard 809 < identity 1,124 < translate 1,475 < colorswap 2,370 (relabel binding is the costliest local operation). The NL half of the S3 law awaits genuinely nonlocal tasks (dev-30 correspondence families). Data: `runs/ablation/`.

- **2026-07-29 (stability + knees: the pre-registered S3 verdict)** — 100/100 rows (5 families × {1e-5, 1e-4} × 8 seeds + {3e-5, 3e-4} × 2 seeds; TPU 4-wide; teardown verified, zero TPUs). **(1) S3 adjudication per the pre-registration (same-day §7, written before data):** the split f = A/(I+A) does **not** stratify families (all medians ≈ 0.44–0.63 ≈ ½) and is seed-volatile (spreads ±0.18–0.40) ⇒ **substitution degeneracy dominates: the two currencies are interchangeable in this regime — the (I_local, A_nonlocal) split FAILS its stability test on constructed families (from-scratch, d=12).** The accuracy-qualified **envelope of the total** I+A does stratify families ⇒ per the pre-registered middle branch, **S3 weakens to the total**, and the locality-CLASS claim now rests on the attention-absent ablation (queued) + total envelopes. Not the kill branch (the total stratifies). Thesis §6-S3 to be amended accordingly. **(2) Knee correction (supersedes the 1b bracket):** with 8 seeds, binding families break at 1e-4 (checkerboard 1/8, colorswap 0/8, translate 0/8 exact) — **knees lie in (3e-5, 1e-4), lower than the (1e-4, 1e-3) stated after 1b**; identity and constfill remain exact through 3e-4. **(3) Translate is price-fragile at any β** (3/8 exact even at 1e-5) — the family's seed-flakiness under pricing is now quantified. Per-seed totals swing ±300–1100% at fixed (family, β): envelopes (mins over qualified rows) are the only stable frontier objects — reinforcing the 07-29 terminology rule. Data: `runs/final/` (4 shard JSONLs, 100 rows). — `S1_Floors.md` §7 fixes the (I_local, A_nonlocal) definition and names the three identifiability threats BEFORE the stability data lands (running now): substitution degeneracy, kept-spine leakage, β-path dependence. The attention-absent ablation (attn_max_hw=0, 10 rows) is queued as the locality-CLASS discriminator — the graded (I,A) measurement cannot decide minimal demand by itself. — `S1_Floors.md` written (desk, no compute): **(1) Rigor boundary drawn** — scale-cut floors are unavoidably CONDITIONAL (the kept spine is continuous/unpriced; no unconditional bound exists), while **spatial-cut floors are rigorous and computable** — and our ledger does not yet resolve spatial cuts ⇒ the spatial-cut ledger is the missing instrument for S1's theorem-grade half. **(2) Reference scales computed**: identity 103.0 nats content vs envelope 5,617; checkerboard ~4 nats of pattern parameters vs envelope 6,237 ⇒ **SGD learned transport, not computation — the frontier measures learned solvers, not floors**; gap decomposes as coding rent (~0.2 nats/dim, DOMINANT) × field redundancy × optimization gap. **(3) Spatial ICs**: identity/colorswap/constfill = 0 (spatially diagonal), translate ≈ 12.9 nats (one column), checkerboard = O(boundary) — the per-family (spatial, scale) cost signature feeds S3's locality classes directly. **(4) Terminology (binding):** "floor" = analytic lower bound only; measured = "envelope". **(5) Experiments queued**: IR-only probe (kept-capacity measurement), spatial-cut ledger, free-bits envelope-tightening (predict ≥10× drop). Constfill is the closest sandwich (326 vs ~2 nats). Today's stability+knee chain feeds this unchanged. — PI-called halt; full audit in the local archive. **Correction to sweep-1 finding F1:** the family ordering was quoted at fixed β=1e-5, but the S1 estimator is the per-family **envelope** (minimum flux over accuracy-qualified rows across the β grid). Envelope upper-estimates: **constfill 326 (β=1e-3) < identity 5,617 (β=1e-4) < checkerboard 6,237 (β=1e-5) < colorswap 12,438 < translate 13,247**. Identity moves BELOW checkerboard — the "content-entropy ordering" was partly a fixed-β artifact; corrected reading: **completion is neighbor-dependent computation, and its accuracy breaks before further compression — computation structure, not just content entropy, prices the cut.** Frontier protocol amended (binding): floors are quoted only as envelopes; single-β columns are never floors. Regime caveat made standing: all Phase-2 results to date are from-scratch full fits at d=12 — stated as such in every claim; deployment-regime (pretrained + TTT) replications arrive with Phase 3. Re-oriented plan and Aug-15 descope decision point in the archived memo. — **Stability sweep LOST; two infrastructure flaws found and fixed; zero TPUs confirmed.** Chain of events: the 80-row S3 stability sweep ran 4-wide on v5litepod-4 spot (both 8-chip quota buckets are capped — preemptible litepod limit is **4 chips/zone** in us-east1-c; the 2026-07-16 "16 chips" note was a different metric; quota-bump request queued for PI); the 3.5 h wall ceiling proved **undersized** for cold-cache 4-wide (20 rows/shard × >10 min) and killed the sweep mid-run (~03:15); the ceiling's remote-kill SSH had **no timeout and hung ~7 h**; the dead-man's switch fired at 10 h — exposing the critical flaw: **guest `shutdown -h` does NOT stop TPU billing (node stays READY); only node DELETION does** — the prior backstop assumption was wrong; the delayed teardown then deleted the VM before any rescue could run (post-DMS SSH is blocked), destroying the partial JSONLs. Cost of the night ≈ $25–35; science lost = the partial stability rows (re-run ≈ 3.5–5 h, ~$10). **Fixes landed:** every dispatcher `sh()` call now bounded (default 600 s); the ceiling path rescues data BEFORE killing; ceilings must be sized from measured row-times × rows/shard × 1.5. **Open (needs PI/console):** a true unattended billing backstop requires cloud-side scheduled deletion (Cloud Scheduler → TPU delete) or a quota-limited standing budget alarm; until then the rule is procedural — `down` at session end, and any unattended run's ceiling + teardown must fit INSIDE the DMS window with margin. Stability sweep re-run queued with a 6 h ceiling. — **Harsh-β regime characterized; F4's under-convergence hypothesis PARTIALLY REFUTED.** 10/10 rows at β=1e-3 with doubled priced phase (600+600): monotone flux restored ONLY for constfill (326 < 794 nats); transport families still carry MORE flux than at 1e-4 (identity 12.5k vs 5.6k; checkerboard 26.3k; colorswap 27.9k; translate 57.3k) with degraded accuracy (0.875–0.935; identity/constfill stay exact). Reading: at β=1e-3 the toll dominates the objective for transport families — fits never reach compressed solutions; **measured flux at a non-solution is not a frontier point but the frontier's TERMINUS**. The knee for binding families lies in (1e-4, 1e-3); identity/constfill knees beyond 1e-3. **Frontier protocol (binding):** only accuracy-qualified rows contribute frontier points; grids localize knees (add 3e-5, 3e-4) rather than extend into the forbidden regime. (F5) LoO gaps ≈ 0 on all solved constructed rows — these families generalize fully once fit; **the S2 scatter needs memorization-prone tasks (dev-30 / generator-gap), as planned.** (F6) Free-channel blowups reach 5.1e8 attention-nats (translate β=0) — β_nl > 0 is mandatory in every future run. - **2026-07-27** — **C14 (Amendment D) IMPLEMENTED** — priced attention at all scales, "wormhole tolls." Design: attention runs at every scale (32×32 and 16×16 added; `attn_max_hw` 8→32); each site emits a variational message `(μ, log σ)` of width `d_a=6`; the toll `A_s = KL(N(μ,σ)‖N(0,1))` is summed per scale (enc + dec, both at resolution 32/2^s at loop index s) into `StepOutput.flux_attn`; priced by a separate `β_nl` (`beta_flux_nl`, default 0 in Phase 1). **Pricing is at emission**: messages are sampled *before* the attention mixing, so the received signal is a convex combination of tolled codes — by data processing, Σ A_s upper-bounds what crosses the nonlocal channel, the same variational status as the stream ledger I_s. Transport in d_a=6 dims also keeps 1024-token attention affordable. Measured: **params 45,653 → 45,509 at d=12** (−144: the VIB replaced the full-d value/output projections — under the +2–3k envelope); CPU step cost at CI-3a batch (96×32×32) **0.29 → 1.98 s/step** (~20 min/task at 600 steps — acceptable for Phase-1 gates, negligible on TPU); attention flux at init positive and finite at all 5 scales. **Direction probe** (smoke scale, d=8/T=2, 60 steps, same seed/data): β_nl=0 → A explodes to 9.3e7 nats (the free nonlocal channel is the unpriced-path loophole the spec's U-Net contrast predicts — visible in the ledger, exactly as designed); β_nl=1e-3 → A = 5.5e3 nats (ratio ~6e-5). The toll steers; Phase-2 runs must keep β_nl > 0 or monitor A_s free-bits. Tests shipped in the same change (discipline rule 1): `test_c14_attention_flux_all_scales`, `test_c14_ablation_knobs` (absent/coarse-only/all-scales), `test_c14_toll_enters_loss` (loss difference equals the deep-supervision mean of Σ A_s exactly); S9 equivariance test extended to both flux ledgers; all 25 tests green. CI-1/CI-2 unaffected. Status: mechanism [P-M]; S3 benefit stays [H] until Phase-2 stability + ablation. CI-3a triad re-run under C14 launched (regression sentinel); result appended when complete.

## 6. What this buys the paper

The ledger *is* the experimental section's skeleton: §3's rows become the ablation table; [P-M] entries become the motivation section's evidence; the append-only log documents that hypotheses were registered **before** results existed — pre-registration discipline that reviewers reward.
